### 2026-11-02 13:19:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Gasoline Drops to Over 2-Month Low — US gasoline futures fell to $3.04 per gallon, their lowest level since late August, amid hopes for diplomatic efforts to ease Middle East tensions. Iranian negotiators held talks in Doha with Qatari mediators for a third consecutive day on Sunday aimed at reaching an agreement to end the US-Israeli war on Iran. Meanwhile, US Secretary of State Marco Rubio said on Saturday that President Trump had not made a decision on fresh strikes against Iran and that Washington remained open to a diplomatic solution. Additional pressure came from rising inventories and weaker demand. EIA data showed an unexpected 3.8 million-barrel increase in gasoline stocks in the week ended October 23, while gasoline supplied, a proxy for demand, fell by 624,000 bpd to 8.36 million bpd. However, inventories remained around 2% below their five-year seasonal average, while seasonal refinery maintenance and strong diesel production ahead of winter could limit gasoline output.

### 2026-11-02 13:09:50  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Heating Oil Slides to 4-Week Low — US heating oil prices fell to $4.56 per gallon, their lowest in four weeks, as Qatari-mediated talks to resolve the US-Iran conflict helped ease concerns over renewed hostilities. US Secretary of State Marco Rubio also said on Saturday that President Trump had not made a decision on fresh strikes against Iran and that Washington remained open to a diplomatic solution. Prices also declined amid prospects of increased fuel supplies. Europe plans to release 42 million barrels from strategic reserves over three months, half of which will be diesel, while additional Chinese export quotas and higher production elsewhere are expected to improve availability. Potentially higher Russian diesel shipments to the US and global markets could also help ease supply tightness following temporary US Treasury authorization for certain transactions involving Russian diesel. However, supply constraints persist, with inventories remaining 10% below their 5-year seasonal average as of October 23.

### 2026-11-02 13:06:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Ends 0.7% Lower, Snaps 3-Day Winning Streak — The NZX 50 shed 98 points, or 0.7%, to close at 13,699 on Monday, halting gains from the prior three sessions and tracking a decline on Wall Street on Friday due to a sell-off in major tech stocks. Caution also built ahead of the release of New Zealand's Q3 unemployment data due later this week, while traders remained concerned about signals of a possible interest rate hike at the RBNZ's December meeting amid the risk of rising inflation. Disappointing Chinese PMI data also pressured sentiment, as the government's survey showed that factory activity unexpectedly contracted in October, while the private survey showed that manufacturing growth eased. However, moderating oil prices eased inflation concerns. The broader index moved away from its highest level since October 15, touched on Friday, mainly weighed down by materials and healthcare. Notable losers included Fisher & Paykel (-3.2%), Chorus (-2.0%), Freightways Group (-2.0%), and Westpac Banking Corp. (-1.1%).

### 2026-11-02 12:51:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Currency #Importance 1

Rupee Rebounds on RBI Support — The Indian rupee strengthened to around 94.90 per dollar, reversing recent losses as Reserve Bank of India measures to manage foreign exchange demand and ease pressure on the currency provided support. The central bank's special foreign exchange window for state-owned oil refiners has helped limit their reliance on spot-market dollar purchases, while its FCNR(B) deposit scheme has mobilized additional foreign currency inflows. Additionally, easing crude oil prices amid progress in US-Iran negotiations offered some relief to India's import bill, although Middle Eastern supply risks continue to keep energy costs elevated. India could also attract foreign portfolio inflows as investors diversify away from technology-heavy markets amid concerns over AI spending and valuations. However, persistent foreign investor outflows, elevated oil import costs, and firm US Treasury yields remain key risks to a sustained recovery.

### 2026-11-02 12:51:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Imports YoY #Importance 1

Indonesia Import Growth Eases to 6-Month Low — Indonesia's imports rose 18.41% year-on-year to USD 24.23 billion in September 2026, falling short of expectations for a 20.83% surge and slowing from a 19.09% increase in August, marking the softest growth since March. Oil and gas imports grew 23.94% year-on-year to USD 3.27 billion, boosted by higher purchases of oil products (37.01%), while crude oil imports dropped 7.00%. Non-oil and gas imports rose 17.59% year-on-year to USD 20.97 billion, mainly driven by increased purchases of machinery and mechanical appliances and parts thereof (1.32%), electrical machinery and equipment and parts thereof (25.87%), and plastics and plastic products (16.27%). Among trading partners, China was the largest source of non-oil and gas imports, with purchases surging 30.23%, followed by Japan (5.78%), ASEAN (20.98%), and the EU (18.21%). By contrast, imports from the US fell 16.63%. In the first nine months of the year, imports increased 19.67% to USD 210.63 billion.

### 2026-11-02 12:46:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

Indonesia Trade Surplus Tops Estimates — Indonesia's trade surplus stood at USD 3.36 billion in September 2026, exceeding expectations of USD 1.9 billion but narrowing from USD 4.34 billion in the same month last year. Exports rose 11.87% yoy, surpassing forecasts of a 6.45% increase and accelerating from a 6.72% rise in August. Non-oil and gas exports climbed 12.39% from a year earlier to USD 26.64 billion, driven by higher shipments to the US (23.21%), China (11.82%), Japan (5.00%), ASEAN (12.46%), and the EU (3.80%). By contrast, oil and gas exports fell 0.99% to USD 0.95 billion, largely due to a decline in crude oil exports (-100.0%). Meanwhile, imports grew 18.41% yoy, below estimates of 24.23% and slowing from a 20.83% rise in August. Oil and gas imports jumped 23.94% year-on-year to USD 3.27 billion, while non-oil and gas imports rose 17.59% to USD 20.97 billion. In the first nine months of 2026, the country posted a trade surplus of USD 10.61 billion, with exports and imports advancing 5.58% and 19.67%, respectively.

### 2026-11-02 12:38:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Hover Near 1-Week Low — US natural gas prices steadied around $2.90/MMBtu, remaining near a one-week low as persistently mild weather and a recovery in domestic production weighed on the market. Meteorologists forecast that temperatures across much of the country would remain mostly warmer than normal during the first half of November, likely keeping heating demand subdued. At the same time, output across the Lower 48 states recovered to an average of around 113.3 bcfd over the past week, improving domestic supply availability. These factors are outweighing support from strong LNG exports and tightening storage levels. Average feedgas flows to the nine major US LNG export terminals rose to 18.5 bcfd in October, up from 17.9 bcfd in September. Meanwhile, the EIA reported a larger-than-expected increase in inventories for the week ended October 23, bringing total stocks to roughly 1% above the five-year average and 3.8% below year-ago levels.

Indonesia Export Growth Strongest in 5 Months — Indonesia's exports grew 11.87% year-on-year to a four-year high of USD 27.59 billion in September 2026, surpassing forecasts of a 6.45% rise and accelerating from a 6.72% increase in August. It was the fourth consecutive monthly increase in exports and the strongest growth since April. ### 2026-11-02 12:29:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #China #Currency #Importance 1

Offshore Yuan Rallies to Over 4-Year High — The offshore yuan strengthened to around 6.64 per dollar on Monday, extending its winning streak to a fifth consecutive session and reaching its strongest level since June 2022, after the People’s Bank of China announced a shift in its short-term monetary policy framework toward the overnight rate. Under the new framework, the central bank will introduce daily overnight reverse repo operations at a fixed rate of 1.25%, bringing the overnight rate closer to becoming its primary policy anchor and gradually replacing the long-standing seven-day benchmark. The newly disclosed 1.25% rate is below the existing 1.4% seven-day reverse repo rate and broadly in line with market expectations and prevailing borrowing costs. Meanwhile, weak official PMI data highlighted persistent economic headwinds. Manufacturing PMI fell to a three-month low of 49.3 in October 2026 from 50.1 in September. Non-manufacturing PMI also declined to 49.7 from 50.2, signaling a contraction for the sixth time in 2026.

### 2026-11-02 12:28:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Currency #Importance 1

South Korean Won Falls as Overseas Dollar Demand Persists — The South Korean won traded around 1,345 per dollar, reversing recent gains amid persistent demand for overseas assets and foreign equity outflows. South Korean residents' growing investment in foreign securities has become a structural source of dollar demand, as net purchases of overseas securities rose 71.5% month-on-month to $7.09 billion in October, weakening the traditional link between trade surpluses and won appreciation. Additionally, resident foreign-currency deposits reached a record $154.7 billion in September, up $14 billion from the previous month, reflecting increased exporter dollar receipts and funds held for overseas bond investments. ### 2026-11-02 12:12:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Indonesia Inflation Eases Slightly — Indonesia’s annual inflation edged down to 3.23% in October 2026 from September’s three-month high of 3.28%, coming below market expectations of 3.29% and staying within Bank Indonesia’s target range of 1-1/2%-3-1/2%. Price growth slowed for recreation (1.76% vs 1.81% in September), education (1.35% vs 1.43%), and personal care (4.52% vs 7.77%). Meanwhile, cost accelerated for housing (1.06% vs 1.02%), transport (4.87% vs 4.84%), clothing (1.26% vs 1.20%), furnishings (2.39% vs 2.19%), healthcare (2.31% vs 2.24%), and communication (1.91% vs 1.83%). Food inflation hit a five-month peak (4.82% vs 4.37%), reflecting the lingering effects of El Niño disruptions. Core inflation, which excludes volatile food and government-administered prices, fell to 2.56% from 2.84% in September, marking a six-month low and falling short of forecasts of 2.75%. Monthly, consumer prices rose 0.22%, slowing from 0.3% in September and undershooting forecasts of 0.25%, but marking a third monthly increase.

### 2026-11-02 12:11:19  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Australia 10Y Yield Rises as RBA Rate Hike Looms — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield rose above 5.1%, rebounding from a two-month low in the previous session as investors positioned ahead of the Reserve Bank of Australia's monetary policy decision. The RBA is expected to raise its cash rate by 25 bps to 4.85%, its highest level in nearly two decades and marking its fifth rate hike this year. The widely expected decision reinforced policymakers’ focus on bringing inflation under control after the annual inflation rate rose to 3.9% in Q3, well above the RBA’s 2–3% target range, while underlying inflation remained elevated at 3.7%. Meanwhile, Governor Michele Bullock described the labour market as “a little tighter” than the RBA had expected, despite the unemployment rate remaining at a five-year high of 4.7% in September. On the economic front, Australia’s Melbourne Institute Monthly Inflation Gauge increased by 0.5% month-on-month in October 2026, picking up from 0.3% in September and marking its fourth consecutive monthly gain.

【China National Energy Administration: Q3 2026 coal-fired generation and coal use fell; stocks adequate for winter peak】China's National Energy Administration said coal-fired power generation and its share declined YoY in Q3 2026, reflecting further progress in the energy low-carbon transition. Recent measures to boost coal production and secure supply have steadied availability; consumption has been relatively stable, narrowing the supply-demand gap and leaving thermal coal inventories in a safe, reasonable range to support winter peak supply. As of Oct. 25, Bohai Rim port coal stocks were 26.92 mln tonnes, up 2.15 mln tonnes YoY; Qinhuangdao held 5.95 mln tonnes, up 0.42 mln. Centrally dispatched power-plant coal stocks were 210 mln tonnes on Oct. 25, equivalent to roughly 35 days of supply, broadly in line with the three-year average.

### 2026-11-02 10:37:01  #市場 #央行 #國際

【光大期貨：利率壓制，黃金還能更低嗎？】金價快速下探、觸發批次止損，我認為是一次止損盤集中出清，並非基本面趨勢徹底轉向，11月存在反彈的可能性，核心錨定中期選舉與 12 月利率路徑兩大主線。從中期選舉維度看，選舉結果將直接重塑市場對美國財政擴張、貿易與地緣政策的定價：若國會形成分治格局，後續大規模財政刺激落地難度抬升，總需求邊際降溫，通脹持續上行的預期被削弱，美債長端收益率易出現回撥，給黃金修復空間；若執政黨取得優勢，市場會重新交易赤字擴張、通脹黏性，利率上行壓力延續，反彈高度受限。同時，選舉前政策不確定性本身會帶來階段性避險溢價，但這一類反彈更多是風險溢價修復，屬於脈衝式行情，持續性依賴選舉結果對於白宮後續政策的推演。另外，此前金價下行的核心驅動是美聯儲年內再加息一次，當前市場已較高程度計價 12 月再加一次 25bp。即便 12 月加息落地，若美聯儲在議息會議釋放“本輪加息終點”訊號，市場會快速交易加息週期結束、降息預期前置，形成典型的利空出盡反彈。反過來，若通脹再度反彈，12 月加息預期進一步強化，本次超賣後的技術反彈只是短暫修復，反彈之後仍會再承壓。整體來看，黃金存在反彈機會，但短期仍宜看作博弈式修復，不是趨勢反轉，反彈視窗集中在選舉落地至12月議息前的資料觀察期，需持續跟蹤通脹與就業資料對利率定價的修正幅度。（光大期貨）

### 2026-11-02 10:36:37  #MKT News #Important #Market Regions #Greater China #Commodities #Energy

【China Q3 coal-fired generation and thermal-coal use decline; coal stocks generally sufficient for winter peak — NEA】The National Energy Administration said on Nov. 2 that China’s coal-fired generation and its share of power output fell in Q3 2026 as the energy transition progresses. Coal-fired generation fell YoY by 4.0% in July, 4.6% in August and 1.9% in September; thermal-coal consumption declined accordingly. After coal’s share of power generation first dipped below 50% in 1H, it fell further to 49.1% at end-September. The share of annual newly added coal-fired generation in total new electricity demand continued to fall, reaching 4.4% at end-September. Stocks: as of Oct. 25 Bohai Rim ports held 26.92 mln t of coal, up 2.15 mln t YoY; Qinhuangdao held 5.95 mln t, up 0.42 mln t YoY. Nationwide centrally dispatched power plants held 210 mln t of coal, equivalent to about 35 days’ supply, roughly in line with the three-year average. The NEA said accelerated coal production and supply measures have steadied supply, narrowed the supply-demand gap and, together with stable consumption and adequate inventories, provide a solid base for winter peak supply.

【三季度煤電發電量及電煤用量持續下降 迎峰度冬煤炭庫存總體有保障】2026年三季度，我國煤電發電量及佔比同比下降，能源低碳轉型取得新進展。隨著近期煤炭增產保供舉措加快實施，煤炭供應穩步恢復，與之同時，煤炭消費相對平穩，供需差進一步收窄，疊加電煤庫存保持安全合理區間，為迎峰度冬保供提供堅實基礎。煤電發電量及佔比同比下降。7、8、9三個月，全國煤電發電量同比分別下降4.0%、4.6%、1.9%，電煤用量相應降低。繼今年上半年國內煤電發電量佔比首次低於50%以下，9月底進一步降至49.1%。煤電年新增發電量佔全社會新增用電量的比例持續下降，9月底降至4.4%。煤炭庫存處於安全合理區間。截至10月25日，環渤海港口存煤2692萬噸，同比增加215萬噸，其中秦皇島港存煤595萬噸，同比增加42萬噸。全國統調電廠存煤保持較高水平，10月25日為2.1億噸，平均可用35天，與近三年平均水平相當。

Hong Kong Stocks Edge Up to Begin the Week — The Hang Seng Index edged up about 0.4%, or 87 points, to open at 23,381 on Monday, extending gains from the previous session, led by technology stocks. Banking shares also remained in focus as China’s major state-owned lenders reported their strongest quarterly profit growth in years, with Q3 earnings rising 6.4%–10.8% on stabilising margins and lower deposit costs. Notable movers included Tencent (2.4%), Xiaomi (2.9%), AIA Group (0.6%) and Semiconductor Manufacturing (0.9%). On the downside, Lenovo (-1.1%), Genscript Biotech (-1.6%) and Wuxi Biologics (-1.3%) declined. On the data front, Hong Kong’s preliminary Q3 GDP figures are due tomorrow, offering a fresh read on the domestic economic backdrop.

### 2026-11-02 10:26:08  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Reverse Repo Rate #Importance 1

PBoC Moves Toward Overnight Rate as Main Policy Anchor — The People’s Bank of China will shift its short-term monetary policy framework toward the overnight rate from Monday, introducing daily overnight reverse repo operations at a fixed rate of 1.25%. The move brings the overnight rate closer to becoming its main policy anchor, replacing the long-standing seven-day benchmark. The operations will use fixed-rate, quantity-based tenders to improve short-term interest rate management. The 1.25% rate, disclosed for the first time, is below the existing 1.4% seven-day reverse repo rate and broadly aligns with market expectations and actual borrowing costs. Under the revised framework, other liquidity tools, including temporary repo and reverse repo operations, will also be priced off the overnight rate, with existing spreads unchanged. Seven-day operations will continue but shift to fixed-quantity tenders with multiple winning bid rates, mainly serving liquidity needs around tax payment periods and month-ends.

【China's National Data Administration to map AI data demand, push industry dataset registration】On Oct. 28 the National Data Administration convened a meeting of industry high-quality dataset chain-master units, instructing them to map artificial intelligence data needs and accelerate dataset development. Chain-master units were told to base work on their own needs, proactively identify upstream and downstream external demand, and follow a demand-led approach for precise dataset construction. They were directed to pilot benefit-sharing and commercial-return mechanisms, compile industry data maps, promote distributed aggregation of industry data and register datasets with the national dataset management service system. The administration also ordered establishment of application-driven data feedback loops to enable data return and iterative optimization, continuous user feedback and dynamic dataset updates, and stronger industry-chain coordination to broaden cooperation and build ecosystem capacity.

【韓國擬2028年年中推行股票T+1結算】股票賣出資金到賬時長將由兩個營業日縮短至一個營業日。韓國交易所、韓國證券存管院、韓國證券金融公司、韓國金融投資協會2日釋出《證券市場結算週期縮短路線圖（草案）》。目標落地時間為2028年年中。實施後，沿用50餘年、自1972年啟用的T+2結算制度將改為T+1。T指股票成交當日，結算週期即從成交日到資金與證券實際交割完成的營業日天數。外資的時差問題、早間外匯兌換環節被視作落地阻礙。美國投資者實際上需要在單日之內完成交易確認與外匯兌換。日本目前仍實行T+2制度，部分希望統一交易時序的全球基金暗示，或將削減對韓投資。

【Kiuchi says cautious on declaring escape from deflation, will focus on supply-demand gap】Kiuchi told reporters after a Cabinet meeting on Nov. 2 the government will "carefully decide" whether to declare an end to deflation, weighing underlying price trends and background factors. He said the core basis for judgment is the supply-demand gap and authorities will "carefully watch" whether it can remain positive, including its future trajectory. Kiuchi added that escaping deflation requires not only ending sustained price declines but also ensuring prices do not revert. Referring to the Bank of Japan's Oct. 30 comment that core inflation is "basically in line" with the 2% target, he said he expects the Bank of Japan to continue to operate policy appropriately to achieve price stability and that he "respects" the bank's approach to accelerating ETF sales.

China Stocks Start November Near 2025 Lows — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.4% to 3,613 on the first trading day of November, while the Shenzhen Component fell 0.1% to 10,819, with both benchmarks hovering near their lowest levels in more than a year as weak official PMI data underscored persistent economic headwinds. Manufacturing PMI fell to a three-month low of 49.3 in October 2026 from 50.1 in September, as domestic demand weakened following the Golden Week boost. Non-manufacturing PMI slipped to 49.7 from 50.2, signaling a contraction in services activity for the sixth time this year. Meanwhile, Chinese financial regulators reportedly met with banks and securities firms on Monday to discuss further measures to support the stock market. These moves would add to stabilization efforts launched last month, which have yet to reverse the downturn amid weak domestic demand and concerns over AI earnings. Agricultural Bank of China (-1.1%) and Bank of China (-1.0%) fell, while CATL (1.7%) and Wuliangye Yibin (3.4%) gained.

【阿根廷媒體稱阿情報機構協助弗拉維奧·博索納羅出逃】據阿根廷11月1日媒體報道，阿根廷國家情報秘書處的一名情報人員策劃並協助了在巴西總統選舉中落敗的自由黨候選人弗拉維奧·博索納羅前往厄瓜多。 阿根廷媒體《第12頁報》當天報道說，弗拉維奧·博索納羅及其家人於當地時間10月27日晚經由米西奧內斯省的坦克雷多·內維斯國際大橋邊境口岸入境阿根廷，隨後登上一架私人飛機前往厄瓜多瓜亞基爾市。 報道還說，阿根廷國家情報秘書處的一名情報人員策劃了此次行動，該情報人員曾在美國駐阿根廷大使館工作。 據阿根廷多家媒體此前報道，阿根廷總統顧問聖地亞哥·卡普託參與協助弗拉維奧·博索納羅出逃，並在其中發揮了重要作用。美國國務卿魯比奧10月31日稱，美國政府“並未直接參與”任何協助弗拉維奧·博索納羅離境赴美的行動。 在10月25日結束的巴西總統選舉第二輪投票中，勞工黨候選人、現任總統盧拉擊敗弗拉維奧·博索納羅贏得連任。巴西聯邦最高法院10月26日裁定禁止弗拉維奧·博索納羅離境。10月28日，弗拉維奧·博索納羅與妻子和兩個女兒抵達美國佛羅里達州。 阿根廷總統府暫未對此事作出公開回應。(新華社)

### 2026-11-02 09:58:20  #其他

【農業農村部市場與資訊化司負責同志就第二十三屆中國國際農產品交易會籌備情況答記者問】問：本屆農交會與往屆相比創新和特點是什麼？
答：農交會已成功舉辦了22屆，我們始終聚焦服務產業，堅持務實創新，與往屆相比有以下3方面特點。
一是新產品新技術呈現更多豐富成果。本屆農交會彙集良種良法、智慧農機、綠色農資等創新產品，集中展示近年來農業農村領域的新技術、新成果，直觀感受新品種、新裝備落地應用成效。中國農業科學院、中國熱帶農業科學院、中國水產科學研究院集體亮相，攜帶500多種新產品、新技術、新裝備參展，覆蓋果蔬加工、食物營養、功能性食品等領域，同期將舉辦新品首秀髮布活動。
二是交流互鑑涉及更多議題。本屆農交會以品牌產品展示“三農”工作的輝煌成就，以權威釋出展現農業農村現代化發展方向，以深入交流把握市場機遇和自身定位，為各地各有關單位謀劃未來發展提供思路和借鑑。期間，圍繞品牌培育、供應鏈打造、欠發達地區產業發展、智慧農業建設等議題，組織專題活動開展深入研討。
三是參展企業覆蓋更多領域。本屆農交會國內參展企業較上屆增長26.1%，首次參加農交會的企業佔比約為30%，覆蓋農產品加工上下游、金融保險服務等多個領域。中國工商銀行、中國電信將作為戰略合作伙伴，深度參與本屆農交會，金融助農、數智賦能更加有力，透過農交會搭建起中國和世界各國農業交流合作的橋樑。

【RatingDog: China manufacturing PMI 52.0 in October, 11th month of expansion】RatingDog's China manufacturing PMI was 52.0 in October, the 11th consecutive month in expansion. Output growth accelerated sharply, the fastest since April, while new orders expanded at a pace roughly unchanged from September, extending the expansion cycle toward 18 months. New export orders expanded for a fourth month but slowed to a three-month low. Input-price inflation eased versus September and fell below the year-to-date average; factory-gate prices fell again, the second decline in three months, amid intensified competition. Overall, manufacturing entered Q4 with steady expansion and strong output and order momentum, though renewed price cuts and weaker business confidence are risk factors to monitor.

### 2026-11-02 09:45:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 2

Japan Manufacturing Growth Confirmed at 7-Month Low — The S&P Global Japan Manufacturing PMI was confirmed at 53.3 in October 2026, matching preliminary estimates, but declining from 54.1 in September. It was the softest expansion in the manufacturing sector since March, as firms recorded slower increases in output and new orders, with both growing at their weakest rates in seven months. Employment continued to increase, with the pace of job creation remaining among the fastest seen over the past decade. Purchasing activity increased at the weakest pace in six months. Meanwhile, suppliers’ delivery times lengthened substantially. On the price front, input cost inflation accelerated for the first time in five months, while output costs continued to rise, though inflation eased to a six-month low. Looking ahead, business sentiment remained positive, ticking up slightly.

Silver Rebounds from Near One-Year Low — Silver climbed toward $50 an ounce on Monday, recovering modestly from near one-year lows as declining oil prices eased concerns over inflation and potential interest rate increases. Oil prices declined amid signs of ongoing diplomatic efforts to prevent renewed US military action against Iran following the midterm elections. President Trump had previously threatened to resume strikes against Iran after the vote, while Tehran warned of broader retaliation. Meanwhile, investors are awaiting this week’s closely watched US jobs report for further insight into the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy outlook, with markets currently assigning around a 75% probability of a rate hike in December. Last week, silver faced significant selling pressure as persistent concerns over energy-driven inflation strengthened expectations of further Fed rate increases. A broader global equity selloff fueled by AI concerns also prompted investors to liquidate silver holdings to cover losses in other assets.

Meanwhile, investors are awaiting this week’s closely watched US jobs report for further insight into the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy outlook, with markets currently assigning around a 75% probability of a rate hike in December. Last week, gold faced significant selling pressure as persistent concerns over energy-driven inflation strengthened expectations of further Fed rate increases. A broader global equity selloff fueled by AI concerns also prompted investors to liquidate gold holdings to cover losses in other assets.

### 2026-11-02 09:41:23  #MKT News

【Second‑review draft of anti‑cross‑border corruption law strengthens work principles】The second‑review draft of the anti‑cross‑border corruption law, submitted to the 25th session of the 14th National People’s Congress Standing Committee on the 2nd, expands the statute’s legislative purpose and strengthens principles guiding anti‑cross‑border corruption work. The draft adds a provision to “promote building a fair and reasonable global anti‑corruption governance system” and specifies that “anti‑cross‑border corruption work shall strictly follow the Constitution and the law, observe legal procedures, and respect and protect human rights.” It sets rules on agencies’ use of information technology in anti‑corruption work and requires that “relevant agencies and their staff bear legal confidentiality obligations for state secrets, work secrets, commercial secrets, personal privacy and personal information learned during supervision and investigation.” The draft further requires domestic firms engaged in cross‑border operations to, based on company size, business scope and risk controls, reasonably establish integrity and compliance departments or personnel.

【海灣國家擔憂美以再襲伊朗引發地區大戰】海灣國家擔憂，倘若美以再次襲擊已經做好更充分準備的伊朗，或將在整個地區引爆破壞性更強的戰爭，而收拾殘局的重擔將落到它們身上。阿聯酋總統外交顧問安瓦爾·加爾賈什週日在巴林舉辦的國際戰略研究所（IISS）麥納麥對話會上表示：“我們正被捲入一場危機，而我們曾付出巨大外交努力，試圖避免這場危機。我們顯然正在尋求一條政治出路。”特朗普多次表態，美國和以色列會在伊朗“完成任務”。特朗普已排除在美國週二中期選舉前發動新一輪打擊，但在和平談判陷入僵局的情況下，不排除後續重啟攻擊的可能。新一輪衝突會讓已經歷經數月戰火的地區承受更多創傷。伊朗已經向海灣國家發射數千枚導彈與無人機，打擊目標包含石油設施、機場以及發電廠。襲擊造成數十名平民傷亡，地區經濟劇烈動盪。國際貨幣基金組織預測，沙特經濟今年將收縮3.3%，科威特、卡達的經濟降幅預計更大。英國皇家聯合軍種研究所國際安全副研究員穆罕默德·巴沙稱：“我沒有聽到任何一位海灣國家官員或平民支援美以再度打擊伊朗，但多數人認為新一輪襲擊迫在眉睫。他們認為伊朗這一輪會採取更強硬的反擊，不再遵守此前的交戰默契。”

South Korean Shares Rebound on Record Exports — The benchmark KOSPI rose nearly 1% to around 5,780 on Monday, recovering losses from the previous session as record export figures highlighted strong underlying demand for South Korean semiconductors. Exports surged 93.7% year-on-year to a record $115.3 billion in October, led by a 259% jump in semiconductor shipments to $56.4 billion, reinforcing the strength of the country's export-driven technology sector. SK Square gained nearly 1%, while other notable movers included Hyundai Motor (1.1%), Hanwha Aerospace (3.7%), Hyundai Mobis (4.8%), HD Hyundai Heavy Industries (6.4%), LG Electronics (1.6%) and LS Electric (4.7%). Meanwhile, concerns over the sustainability of AI investments continued to weigh on global technology stocks following disappointing earnings from major US tech companies and lower-than-expected capital expenditure forecasts. ### 2026-11-02 09:19:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Ireland Factory Growth Remains Robust — The AIB Ireland Manufacturing PMI edged down to 55.2 in October 2026 from 55.5 in September. Still, the reading continued to signal a robust improvement in overall business conditions. Output rose solidly, although growth slowed from September's four-and-a-half-year high. New orders also increased sharply, with export orders extending their growth streak to nine straight months. Backlogs of work rose at the slowest pace since July. Manufacturers continued hiring to meet stronger demand and boost capacity, with job creation reaching a three-month high. Purchasing activity also grew at its fastest pace since May, partly reflecting efforts to build safety stocks, as pre-production inventories recorded their strongest increase since January 2023. Meanwhile, input cost inflation accelerated to a four-month high, driven by higher energy, fuel and raw material prices. Output charges also rose at their fastest pace since June. Finally, confidence in the year-ahead outlook improved further.

【一批新技術新產品將亮相 2026年“網際網路之光”博覽會開幕】今天，作為世界網際網路大會烏鎮峰會的重要活動板塊，2026年“網際網路之光”博覽會正式開幕。來自54個國家和地區的671家企業，展示了1000餘項新產品新技術。本屆博覽會以“智慧互聯、智見未來”為主題，設定空天地海·新連線、智慧湧現·新空間、人機共生·新入口等6大主題展區，全方位展現“智慧網際網路”的發展成果。其中，衛星網際網路、6G等一批網際網路的新技術新產品在博覽會上首次亮相。本屆博覽會還採用場景化的方式，集中展示了百餘款面向工業製造、康養醫療、文娛教育的具身智慧機器人，呈現科技創新與產業創新深度融合、共同賦能生產生活的高價值應用場景。今年，“網際網路之光”博覽會突出數智普惠，增加許多社會觀眾、面向青少年群體展示與活動，讓前沿科技走進千家萬戶。據瞭解，2026年“網際網路之光”博覽會將持續到11月5日，其間將舉辦115場數智創新發布和10場產業合作對接活動。

【China to adopt differentiated land management for facility agriculture】The Ministry of Natural Resources, Ministry of Agriculture and Rural Affairs and National Forestry and Grassland Administration issued joint guidance to apply differentiated, positive‑list land management for facility agricultural land to boost farmers’ incomes, agricultural modernization and rural revitalization. The notice encourages siting facility farmland on unused areas—Gobi, desert, saline‑alkali land—plus existing construction land and idle land, and permits occupation of arable land outside permanent basic farmland. It removes scale limits on production facilities, imposes land‑share and size caps for different types of ancillary facilities, and encourages facility farmland to be used primarily for agricultural production.

### 2026-11-02 09:09:03  #其他

【我國將對設施農用地實行差異化管理】記者今天（2日）從自然資源部獲悉，為了進一步促進農民群眾增產增收，助力農業現代化發展和鄉村振興，自然資源部、農業農村部、國家林草局近日聯合印發通知，要求透過正面清單方式，對設施農業使用土地實行差異化管理。設施農業，簡單說就是利用溫室、大棚、養殖場等設施搞種養殖的農業，用地形式多樣，耕地、園地乃至建設用地都可能用到。本次釋出的《通知》，把設施農業用的土地分成三類。明確要求，沒有破壞耕作層的設施農業，比如小型的農田水利、水肥一體化設施，仍按原來的土地用途管理，不用新辦理用地手續；對耕作層造成破壞、直接為農業生產服務的種植、養殖等生產設施和附屬設施，按設施農用地管理，需要進行備案；沒納入“設施農業使用土地正面清單”的設施，則需要按建設用地進行管理。在選址上，未來將鼓勵設施農用地使用戈壁、荒漠、鹽鹼地等未利用地和存量建設用地、空閒土地，允許佔用永久基本農田以外的耕地。同時，對生產設施規模不作限制，對不同型別的附屬設施實行用地比例和規模上限控制，鼓勵設施農用地更多直接用於農業生產。

【深圳地鐵客運強度連續33個月內地城市居首】今年前三季度，深鐵集團統籌推動建設、運營、經營三大板塊協同發力，核心運營指標居全國前列。地鐵客流成為觀察城市活力最直觀的視窗——今年暑運，深圳地鐵日均客運量達1009.6萬人次，歷史首次突破千萬大關，兩個月累計運送乘客6.26億人次，創歷年同期新高。截至9月21日，單日客流年內已累計86次突破千萬人次、18次突破1100萬人次，全面超越歷年水平。前三季度（截至9月21日），深圳地鐵總客運量24.77億人次，同比增長5.2%。其中，8月客運量、進站量首次登上全國第一，客運強度連續33個月位居內地城市第一，線網密度、公交分擔率、智慧出行指數長期領跑全國。客流的持續攀升，來自軌道線網版圖的不斷生長。今年6月28日，地鐵13號線二期北延段正式開通，深圳軌道交通線網規模增至18條共計653.7公里線路。2026年，深鐵集團同步推進2條鐵路、4條城際及11條（段）地鐵共計558.64公里“三鐵”線路加快建設，穗莞深城際機場至前海段已全線軌通，“軌道上的都市圈”加速成型。(深圳釋出)

Australian Shares Start Week Lower as RBA Decision Looms — Australian stocks fell 26 points, or 0.3%, to 8,883 in early trade on Monday, reversing recent gains as tech, non-energy minerals, and healthcare stocks fell. Caution prevailed ahead of Tuesday’s Reserve Bank interest rate decision, with the central bank expected to raise its cash rate for the fifth time this year to 4.85% amid persistent inflationary pressure. In key trading partner China, official data showed an unexpected drop in manufacturing during October, partly reflecting the impact of the Golden Week break. However, losses were capped by fresh data showing a September rebound in Australia's building permits, driven by a recovery in private-sector dwellings excl. houses. In the U.S., stock futures strengthened ahead of further corporate earnings, jobs data, and the midterm elections. BHP Group fell 1.1% while Rio Tinto lost 1.4%. Gold miners like Northern Star (-3.9%) and Evolution Mining (-2.5%) also slipped. In contrast, the four major banks rose between 0.2% and 1.6%.

### 2026-11-02 09:00:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Vietnam #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Vietnam Manufacturing Growth Picks Up — Vietnam’s S&P Global Manufacturing PMI rose to 52.3 in October 2026, picking up from a three-month low of 51.9 in the previous month. Manufacturing output expanded for another month, supported by stronger customer demand and continued growth in new orders. However, the pace of expansion eased to a six-month low, with some manufacturers reporting that difficulties sourcing raw materials had constrained production. Meanwhile, supplier delivery times lengthened to their greatest extent since April, largely due to higher fuel costs and adverse weather conditions. On the price front, input cost inflation rose for a second straight month to its fastest pace since June, but remained well below the spike recorded following the outbreak of the Middle East war. Manufacturers subsequently raised selling prices at a faster pace. Lastly, business confidence climbed to an eight-month high, reflecting optimism over improved raw material availability and further strengthening in market conditions.

【中信證券：中選對美股影響幾何？】中信證券研報稱，2026年11月3日美國中期選舉在即，我們圍繞“中選―市場”的敘事展開三層分析。其一，歷史覆盤顯示，1946―2022年20次中選後一年標普500回報全部為正、均值+14.4%，且三分類下組間差異遠小於組內離散，“兩院歸屬”對美股方向缺乏獨立定價力。其二，選情方面，預測市場與民調資料一致將“民主黨控制兩院”定價為基準情景（Polymarket約68%、Kalshi約65%），分裂國會為次要替代，但參議院席位測算表明民主黨需淨增4席、且7個Toss-Up州中多數為共和黨現持席位，疊加共和黨在黨組織現金與廣告投放上的優勢，共和黨仍存在守住參議院的現實路徑。其三，兩黨政策分歧顯著、共識有限，行業影響更多取決於“政策能否落地”的確定性——分裂國會對應政策順風最弱，兩黨掌控兩院時其分列政策才構成高彈性的方向性敞口。

### 2026-11-02 08:17:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #MI Inflation Gauge MoM #Importance 1

Australia Inflation Gauge Accelerates Ahead of RBA Meeting — Australia’s Melbourne Institute Monthly Inflation Gauge rose 0.5% month-on-month in October 2026, up from a 0.3% increase in the previous month and marking a fourth straight monthly gain. The stronger reading highlights persistent price pressures ahead of the Reserve Bank’s policy meeting later this week, where markets widely expect a fifth rate hike this year. Annual headline inflation accelerated to a six-month high of 4.3% in September, remaining well above the RBA’s 2-3% target range. RBA Governor Michele Bullock has cautioned that inflation may stay elevated longer than anticipated, while Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter recently indicated policymakers have “not quite yet” done enough to return inflation sustainably to target, reinforcing expectations of further policy tightening.

### 2026-11-02 08:15:40  #其他

【國家資料局召開行業高質量資料集鏈主工作座談會，深化高質量資料集建設“以需定供”“以用促建”】10月28日上午，國家資料局組織召開行業高質量資料集建設鏈主工作座談會，總結鏈主工作進展成效，交流經驗做法，研究部署下一步重點工作。國家資料局黨組成員、副局長夏冰出席會議並講話。會上，來自教育教學、醫療衛生、科學研究、工業製造、生物製造、農業農村、智慧能源、交通運輸、氣象服務等9個領域的21家鏈主單位代表，圍繞高質量資料集建設、技術工具研發、應用賦能等方面交流經驗做法，分享典型案例。會議強調，行業高質量資料集建設要堅持“以需定供、以用促建、以評提質”原則，重點做好以下工作。一是摸清人工智慧用數需求。鏈主單位要立足自身需求，主動拓展產業鏈上下游等外部需求，堅持需求導向，精準推進資料集建設。二是發揮牽引帶動作用。鏈主單位要先行探索建立利益分配和商業回報機制，梳理行業資料地圖，推動行業資料分散式匯聚，引導本行業領域的資料集登記至國家資料集管理服務系統。三是強化應用反饋。依託實際應用場景，建立資料迴流和迭代最佳化機制，持續收集使用者反饋，推動資料集動態更新，不斷提升資料與應用場景的適配性。四是加強產業鏈協同。發揮鏈主單位“聚鏈”作用，聯動產業鏈上下游單位，拓展合作範圍，推動產業生態建設。五是推動設施接入。各鏈主單位要積極接入國家資料基礎設施，依託設施促進資料集規模化流通和高效利用。六是最佳化測評服務。 推動資料集質量測評向工具化、訂單化模式轉變，縮短測評週期、降低測評成本，提升測評效率和交付能力。

【華爾街準備迎接美國國債發行多年來最大調整】美國財政部長斯科特·貝森特近幾個月出人意料地展現出干預債券市場、推動收益率下降的意願，如今華爾街正準備迎接美國國債發行指引自2024年初以來規模最大的一次調整。許多交易商預計，美國財政部將在季度政策宣告中修改相關指引。兩年多來，該指引一直表明中長期國債發行將保持穩定。週三的宣告若出現變化，可能為2027年削減期限最長的國債發行鋪平道路，同時增加國庫券和較短期限國債的招標規模，以此加以平衡。一些機構正在為更激進的舉措做準備。花旗集團預計，財政部將宣佈從本月開始削減20年期和30年期國債發行，這實際上將違背上一份所謂季度再融資宣告中給出的指引。在上一輪計劃公佈僅一週後，貝森特便出人意料地擴大了對存量國債的回購，此舉被視為對收益率飆升的回應。

### 2026-11-02 08:06:15  #央行 #其他

【貸款利率迎來新變化 “雙錨改革”帶來哪些影響？】最近，央行宣佈貸款利率要從單一參考LPR定價向更多元的定價基準轉型。現在，央行行長說以後貸款利率要加入一個新的錨：DR利率。DR利率就是銀行這類存款機構之間借款成交出來的利率，也就是說，DR利率是一筆一筆交易實打實成交出來的，每天都在變。市場上錢很多，DR利率就往下走；市場資金緊了，DR利率立刻往上抬。它屬於“成交型”利率，能更真實反映銀行體系內的流動性狀況和資金鬆緊程度。DR利率靈敏、變化快，適合大中型企業貸款，需要銀行和企業一對一談合同。比如工廠經常要週轉資金，貸款就可以商量錨定DR利率。對企業來說，使用DR能夠更及時、準確感受市場資金成本變化，以更市場化的方式開展投融資決策。對銀行來說，使用DR可以更方便地依照資金成本合理定價。而LPR穩定性較好，房貸、個人貸款、小微企業貸款，繼續用LPR可能更適合。“雙錨改革”就是讓利率傳導更順暢，央行的政策訊號可以更快傳遞到實體經濟，市場化程度更高，也更能滿足不同人群的需求。（央視新聞）

【專家稱政策利率“換錨” 不等於降息】政策利率從原來的7天期逆回購操作利率1.4%切換為隔夜逆回購操作利率1.25%，並不等同於降息。光大證券固定收益首席分析師張旭表示，從期限結構看，較長期限品種的利率通常高於較短期限品種。自2023年初以來，DR007（銀行間7天期回購利率）平均較DR001高約18個基點，此前7天期逆回購操作利率為1.4%，與隔夜逆回購操作利率相差15個基點，兩組利差方向一致、量級接近。顯然，隔夜逆回購操作利率低於7天期逆回購操作利率與正常的利率期限結構相符，不能據此將其解讀為降息15個基點。 中國人民銀行的這一系列公開市場操作調整都從11月2日起實施。天風證券首席固收分析師譚逸鳴預計，隨著隔夜逆回購操作頻率提升，11月資金利率執行有望趨於平穩，資金面維持均衡偏松狀態。（證券日報）

New Zealand Stocks Start the Week Lower — The NZX 50 dropped 95 points, or 0.7%, to 13,702 in Monday morning trade, erasing gains from the prior three sessions and tracking a decline on Wall Street on Friday due to a selloff in major tech stocks. Caution also built ahead of the release of New Zealand's Q3 unemployment data due later this week, after the jobless rate posted its highest reading since Q3 2015 in Q2. Traders also worried over signals of a possible interest rate hike at the RBNZ's December meeting amid the central bank's efforts to combat rising inflation. A contraction in China's manufacturing sector also pressured sentiment, while traders continued to monitor developments in the Middle East and their impact on oil prices. The broader index moved away from its highest level since October 15, touched on Friday, mainly weighed down by energy, financials, and real estate stocks. Among early losers were Fletcher Building (-4.6%), Infratil (-1.8%), Meridian Energy (-1.3%), and Westpac Banking Corp. (-1.2%).

### 2026-11-02 06:39:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 2

Australia Manufacturing PMI Revised Slightly Lower — The S&P Global Australia Manufacturing PMI was revised slightly lower to 49.6 in October 2026 from preliminary estimates of 49.7, unchanged from a 21-month low of 49.6 in September. It was the second straight month of contraction in the manufacturing sector, as new orders fell at the steepest pace since December 2024, driving a solid decline in output. Meanwhile, overseas demand fell for another straight month, though at a slower rate. Purchasing activity continued to fall in line with new orders, with delivery times worsening further. On the price front, input cost inflation accelerated due to higher energy, raw material, supplier, and shipping costs. In response to rising input prices, output costs rose for the first time in five months, as firms opted to pass on their increased cost burdens to clients. Looking ahead, business sentiment remained positive but below its long-run average.

### 2026-11-02 06:15:16  #公司 #市場

【滬深兩市公司三季報折射經濟向新而行】權威資料顯示，截至10月31日，滬深兩市共有5224家上市公司披露了2026年三季報。上述公司前三季度共實現營業收入56.6萬億元，淨利潤5.1萬億元，同比分別增長5.8%、8.5%，規模及增速均創2022年以來新高；滾動淨資產收益率（ROE）為7.9%。其中，73%的滬深上市公司實現盈利。 分板塊看，滬深主機板上市公司淨利潤同比分別增長1.1%、18.0%，均實現正增長；科創板、創業板上市公司同比分別增長474.6%（剔除長鑫科技後增長106.9%）、22.7%。 市場分析人士認為，人工智慧產業（AI）加快發展、上游能源資源價格上漲、出口高增三條主線驅動業績快速增長，上市公司利潤增量正在從傳統權重行業向新經濟與全球定價環節遷移。（中證報）

### 2026-11-02 05:33:21  #MKT News #DXY #Impact bearish #EURUSD #Impact bullish

【Persistent inflation likely to prompt RBA fifth rate hike this year】The Reserve Bank of Australia is expected on Tuesday to deliver its fifth hike this year, raising the cash rate by 25bps to 4.85% — an 18‑year high — with money markets pricing roughly an 80% probability. The decision will include updated economic forecasts; Governor Bullock will hold a press conference one hour after the announcement. AMP economists Diana Mousina and My Bui say inflation remains well above target, has surprised on the upside with no clear sign of easing, and caution that failing to hike could damage RBA credibility and entrench higher inflation expectations. They point to domestic capacity constraints and a global energy shock as key inflation drivers. A hike would leave the RBA notably more hawkish than peers, after the U.S., euro area and Japan central banks recently held rates and the RBNZ paused unexpectedly last week.

### 2026-11-01 19:09:18  #MKT News #Commodities #Industrial Metals

【China issues national standards for intelligent steel manufacturing to standardize system design】The State Administration for Market Regulation published three national standards for intelligent manufacturing in China’s steel sector: Intelligent Manufacturing Scrap Steel Smart Factory Design Requirements; Intelligent Manufacturing Steel Smart Factory Digital Twin System Technical Requirements; and Intelligent Manufacturing Steel Industry Multi-Base Manufacturing Resource Collaborative Control System Technical Requirements. The standards target scrap-steel plants, digital-twin systems and cross-site coordination and are intended to support a unified advanced-manufacturing technical requirements framework for China’s steel industry. They address long production processes, numerous operations and complex interfaces, and persistent issues including inconsistent design specifications across plants, duplicated system development, poor data interoperability and difficulty coordinating resources across bases. All three standards mandate a layered, decoupled architecture that defines functional roles and technical boundaries at each layer to standardize intelligent-system design and implementation.

【“銀十”跑贏“金九”：上海10月二手房網籤同比大漲33%，“市場止跌回暖訊號已經明確”】上海“銀十”樓市成績單出爐。上海市房地產交易中心官網“網上房地產”資料顯示，10月上海二手房（含商業）累計網籤24653套，不僅超越“金九”約2.35萬套的成交規模，同比更是大幅上漲33%，創下近六年同期成交新高。從單日表現來看，除國慶假期單日網籤量在百套徘徊外，整月市場活躍度持續在高位執行。資料顯示，10月共有7個交易日二手房單日網籤量突破千套，且成交高點持續重新整理。其中，10月17日單日網籤成交1631套，超過10月11日1615套的成交量，成為10月單日成交峰值。這也是今年上海二手房第四次單日網籤突破1600套，僅次於4月11日1632套、5月10日1664套的年內高點。月末，市場再度迎來翹尾行情，10月31日單日二手房網籤成交量再度拉昇至1505套。從月度走勢來看，上海二手房市場韌性持續顯現。自今年3月起，上海二手房成交量已連續第8個月站穩2.3萬套以上，其中3月二手房網籤量更是突破3.1萬套。（澎湃新聞）

### 2026-11-01 17:56:59  #觀點

【中信證券：四種配置思維來應對當下的市場】中信證券提出四種配置思維來應對當下的市場。一是穩健回報策略，核心是圍繞高股息或具備供給出清潛力的行業，在需求線索混沌的階段聚焦供給的變化。二是預期演繹策略，類似於“2022年的航空和酒店”，找到市場對基本面一致看空，持倉比例極低的板塊，拿到最終政策變化的時點再兌現，只做估值修復博弈。三是海外利率回落策略，把北美AI投融資預期修正、海外長端利率相應回落作為宏觀前提，待市場修正EPS預期後，看非AI領域的製造業出海和資源股能否走出短期需求抑制。四是賽道熱度+籌碼出清策略，類似於找尋“2022年的戶儲和逆變器”，以新領域需求的超預期爆發作為抄底科技的時機，尋找機構和活躍資金能夠共振的領域，而非簡單博弈“無機”。中信證券認為四種策略都有獲取相對收益的潛力，適合不同收益-風險偏好的資金，市場的資金面壓力決定了哪類策略階段性佔優。

【中金公司國內策略首席李求索：A股市場估值已具備較好投資吸引力，中期向好趨勢未變】近期，A股市場在外部輸入性風險與資金面壓力下經歷明顯回撥，市場情緒低落。然而，剛剛披露結束的2026年三季報卻展現出另一番景象：全部上市公司前三季度淨利潤同比增長8.2%，跑贏同期名義GDP增速；同時，滬深300前向市盈率跌破過去十年均值，股息率升至3.4%。一面是極具吸引力的歷史底部估值，另一面是AI產業鏈與上游資源品展現出的強勁盈利韌性。面對基本面與資金面的顯著背離，中金公司研究部國內首席策略分析師李求索在接受《每日經濟新聞》記者採訪時明確表示，當下市場已反應過度悲觀預期，A股市場長期穩進的趨勢沒有改變。“我國政策空間大、根基牢、發展潛力足、較多產業領域在全球具備競爭優勢，A股上市公司作為我國優秀企業代表，基本面中期向好趨勢仍在延續”，李求索最後強調，堅實的基本面支撐疊加極具吸引力的估值，讓他對A股市場中長期發展前景依然樂觀。（每經）

【Chinese regulators to meet Nov. 2 to promote use of capital-market support tools】Securities and fund firms have been notified that the People's Bank of China (PBOC) and the China Securities Regulatory Commission (CSRC) will meet on Nov. 2 to study promoting the use of capital-market support tools. On the same day the PBOC, the National Financial Regulatory Administration (NFRA) and the CSRC will convene major banks to discuss optimizing the stock-repurchase re-lending facility. The stock-repurchase re-lending facility, established by a joint PBOC–NFRA–CSRC notice on Oct. 18, 2024, is one of two monetary-policy tools aimed at supporting the capital market; it incentivizes financial institutions to lend to eligible listed companies and major shareholders to fund buybacks and share increases.

【益方生物：Nomelcitinib治療銀屑病三期臨床達主要終點】益方生物公告稱，公司自主研發的靶向TYK2 JH2的新型口服選擇性抑制劑nomelcitinib（D-2570）治療中重度斑塊狀銀屑病的註冊性三期臨床研究達到主要終點和關鍵次要終點。該研究共納入391例患者，治療第16周時，兩個nomelcitinib治療組PASI 90患者比例均超70%、PGA 0/1患者比例均超80%，均顯著高於安慰劑組，且安全性良好。目前該產品頭對頭氘可來昔替尼三期臨床及多個適應症II期臨床正在推進。本次臨床試驗結果不會對公司業績產生重大影響，新藥研發存在不確定性。

### 2026-11-01 13:51:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Saudi Arabia #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 2

Saudi Arabia Q3 GDP Shrinks for 2nd Quarter — Saudi Arabia’s economy shrank 3.8% year-on-year in the third quarter of 2026, after contracting 4.7% in the previous quarter, preliminary estimates showed. It was the second straight quarter of economic contraction, as oil activities slumped 20.4%, following a 24.8% plunge in Q2, due to the ongoing Middle East conflict that disrupted shipping through the Strait of Hormuz. The contraction was also accompanied by a 2.7% decline in government activities, after rising by 0.9% in Q2. However, the economic contraction was partly offset by faster growth in non-oil activities, which rose 1.9%, compared with 0.8% in Q2.

### 2026-11-01 13:51:12  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #DXY #Impact bullish #XAUUSD #Impact bearish #WTI

【Weak Canadian data cast doubt on need for further BOC hikes】Days after a 25bp BOC hike, data show Canadian employment fell 49.6k in August and GDP growth stalled that month, with September likely to contract; the figures imply Q3 annualized GDP near 1.3% versus the BOC’s prior 2% forecast. David Rosenberg, head of Rosenberg Research, said the weak prints challenge expectations of an economic acceleration and questioned whether the central bank can ignore the recent soft data. He added the rate decision looked based on questionable survey signals rather than real activity and that there is no evidence fuel-price rises have passed through to non-energy goods prices.

【地緣定價與需求負反饋拉鋸 能化板塊後市緊盯兩大變數】10月，能化板塊在地緣風險溢價反覆演繹下走出“急漲—高位拉鋸—二次衝高”行情。其中，原油是絕對主線；化工品分化明顯，部分品種價格波動劇烈，亦有品種在需求負反饋下持續磨底。展望11月，多位受訪人士表示，地緣定價與需求負反饋的拉鋸仍將延續，中東局勢與市場需求是影響能化板塊後市的兩大主線。談及原油基本面，國信期貨能化團隊負責人範春華將當前供需概括為“實物供給受地緣與物流約束、全球低庫存託底，高油價反噬需求，風險溢價主導定價”，其本質是“可交付性”溢價，而非總量缺口。需求端，地緣風險導致的負反饋已全面顯現。範春華介紹，中國9月原油加工量同比下跌8.4%；MTO虧損加深、停車增多，浮法玻璃日熔量持續走低；聚酯負荷處於近年低點，江浙織機開工負荷僅53%，終端“旺季不旺”。（期貨日報）

【業內：金價長期走勢將轉向】天府立言金融研究院院長曾剛在接受記者採訪時表示，“今年金價的劇烈波動，本質上是多種驅動力輪流佔據主導的結果。”“進入下半年，局勢逆轉。國際油價上漲推高通脹預期，美國通脹壓力重新抬頭，市場開始押注美聯儲可能進一步加息。實際利率和美元走強，直接抬高了持有不生息資產黃金的機會成本。”對於10月30日的大跌，曾剛也進一步表達了自己的看法：“10月30日，高位擁擠的多頭平倉、止損盤集中觸發，又放大了下跌，形成踩踏。” 曾剛認為，短期看，金價仍受貨幣政策預期和美元走勢主導。在通脹資料未明顯降溫、美聯儲加息預期未消退之前，金價反彈可能受限，價格大機率在低位寬幅震盪。不過連續兩個月下跌、短期跌幅過大，技術性超賣後的反彈也隨時可能出現。投資者不宜盲目抄底，更不宜追殺，分批、小倉位操作更穩妥。長期看，支撐黃金的幾個基礎邏輯並未根本消失。全球地緣政治不確定性、主權債務高企、央行儲備多元化與“去美元化”趨勢，仍會提供中長期支撐。（每經）

### 2026-11-01 10:59:43  #其他

【商務部國際司負責人介紹中國—秘魯自由貿易協定升級議定書生效實施相關情況】《升級議定書》的生效實施，是中國堅定維護以規則為基礎的多邊貿易體制的實際行動，彰顯了中國致力於透過自貿談判，與各國共同維護多邊主義和自由貿易的決心。《升級議定書》的生效實施，還將促進中秘經貿關係，豐富兩國全面戰略伙伴關係內涵，為雙方企業打造更有利的營商環境和貿易投資合作機會，給兩國人民帶來更多實際利益。

一是與時俱進，全面提升規則承諾水平。《升級議定書》在中秘自貿協定的基礎上，以獨立章節形式新增了標準合作、競爭政策、電子商務、全球供應鏈、環境等議題，完善細化了原產地規則、海關程式與貿易便利化、衛生與植物衛生措施、智慧財產權、服務貿易、商務人員臨時入境和投資等原有章節有關規定。其中，全球供應鏈章包括鼓勵多式聯運、促進外貿物流通道和基礎設施建設等，是雙方在自貿協定項下的創新之舉，表明兩國致力於共同維護供應鏈穩定暢通；標準與合格評定合作章成立專門合作機制，提出意向合作清單，將為兩國開展標準互認與合作搭建平臺，降低兩國企業間的貿易成本。

二是互利共贏，不斷改善貿易投資環境。貨物貿易方面，納入電子聯網條款，完成產品特定原產地規則轉版；對通關申報相關的請求答覆、資訊交換、貨物通關、快件通關、易腐貨物通關作出詳細規定；對兩國衛生與植物衛生措施相關法律法規透明度提出更高要求，並設定磋商機制及時解決有關技術性問題。服務投資方面，新增專業服務、運輸服務、文化與視聽服務合作、電信服務等4個具體領域附件，提升服務領域規制與合作水平；納入商務人員入境快速申請程式條款，提高簽證辦理效率；大幅完善投資爭端解決機制，細化最惠國待遇、徵收有關條款內容，為促進相互投資提供有力保障。

三是面向未來，持續深化開放合作內涵。雙方商定將在《升級議定書》生效後兩年內啟動服務貿易和投資負面清單談判，進一步提升兩國間服務貿易和投資開放水平，持續挖掘兩國貿易投資發展潛力。此外，雙方還承諾，未來每兩年審議競爭章節，評估做出更高承諾的可能性。（商務部）

### 2026-11-01 10:59:23  #其他

【中國—秘魯自貿協定升級議定書正式生效】11月1日，《中華人民共和國政府和秘魯共和國政府關於升級〈中華人民共和國政府與秘魯共和國政府自由貿易協定〉的議定書》（以下簡稱《升級議定書》）正式生效。

中秘雙方於2018年11月啟動自貿協定升級談判，於2024年11月14日簽署《升級議定書》。《升級議定書》對原自貿協定進行升級，完善了原產地規則、海關程式與貿易便利化、衛生與植物衛生措施、智慧財產權、服務貿易、商務人員臨時入境和投資等7個協定原有章節，新增了標準與合格評定合作、競爭政策、電子商務、全球供應鏈、環境與貿易等5個章節。雙方還就服務貿易和投資負面清單談判設定未來條款，旨在繼續提高相關領域開放水平。

中秘自貿協定自2010年生效以來，對雙邊經貿合作發揮了重要促進作用。兩國貿易額從2009年的64億美元提高至2025年的504億美元，增長了近7倍。《升級議定書》生效後，將有利於進一步挖掘雙邊貿易投資合作機會，為雙方企業提供更好的營商環境，給兩國經貿關係注入新活力。（商務部）

【歐洲央行行長拉加德看好歐中合作：“我們要一起合作”】歐洲中央銀行行長拉加德近日接受了總檯記者專訪。在談及全球經濟前景與形勢時，拉加德表示，全球經濟在面對諸如俄烏衝突、能源危機等衝擊時展現出了遠超預期的韌性，但負面影響也同時存在，地緣政治局勢的演變以及人們的行動正在阻礙本可實現的經濟發展。拉加德還表示，歐中互為高水平的貨物與服務貿易伙伴，雙方存在共同利益，要一起合作。拉加德稱：“要知道，無論是中國還是歐盟，雙方都很務實。中國在考量自身出口能力與拓展空間時，會著眼全球。眼下，歐盟是極具吸引力的市場。單看歐元區就擁有3.6億人口，我們擁有規模龐大的中產人群，購買力強勁；同時貨物流通水平與貿易開放程度較高，這在當前不少發達經濟體中並不多見。因此歐盟必然會受到中國的重視。同樣，大量歐洲企業也在華落地佈局，設立銷售門店、子公司與製造中心。由此可見，雙方存在共同利益。歐中互為高水平的貨物與服務貿易伙伴。所以，沒錯，我們要一起合作。”（央視網）

【前10個月百強房企銷售總額超2.5萬億元 65家房企單月銷售額環比增長】10月31日晚間，中指研究院、億翰智庫、克而瑞等機構公佈2026年1~10月中國房地產企業銷售業績。據中指研究院統計，2026年1~10月，百強房企操盤銷售額為20016.3億元，權益銷售額為17477.1億元；百強房企銷售總額為25370.6億元，同比降幅較1~9月收窄0.9個百分點。對此，中指研究院企業研究總監劉水分析認為，這主要受到幾方面因素影響：一是政策端發力，“9·29”房貸貼息等政策有效提振購房信心，降低了剛需購房門檻；二是供給端配合，房企抓住視窗集中推盤促銷，優質專案入市加速了觀望客戶的轉化；三是上年低基數的影響。“但企業間銷售表現仍在分化，部分房企依託穩健的資金面、可靠的交付信用，疊加核心城市優質土儲佈局與產品力持續打磨，在市場信心偏弱的環境下更容易獲得購房者青睞，從而實現銷售額同比正增長。”（每經）

今日12大單品的終端零售總價結束此前連續四日的下跌，四款產品價格上漲、四款下跌，漲跌金額完全相抵。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10638元，與昨日持平，結束連續四日下行，仍維持在今年9月中旬以來最低水平。

【韓國10月出口同比增長93.7% 晶片出口暴漲259%】韓國10月出口維持強勁增長，人工智慧熱潮持續拉動這個依賴貿易的經濟體。韓國產業部週日公佈的資料顯示，經工作日差異調整後，出口同比大增76.1%，出口額達1153億美元，上月出口曾創下1211億美元的歷史新高。未經調整口徑下，出口同比上漲93.7%，進口增長36%，貿易順差425億美元；9月出口增幅修正後為83.7%。出口持續強勁，為韓國央行本月加息提供支撐。韓國央行10月維持利率不變，以評估7月、8月連續加息帶來的影響。週二即將公佈的通脹資料，將是韓國央行11月26日利率決議前另一項關鍵參考。半導體仍是拉動增長的主力，晶片出口同比暴漲259%，至564億美元；計算機出口增長497%，石化產品、汽車出口分別上漲80%和18%。

【U.S. report: Iran-linked strikes inflicted severe damage on U.S. bases in Middle East】A U.S. analysis released Oct. 31 says that, as of end‑August, attacks claimed by Iran or Iran‑backed militias damaged or destroyed 26 U.S. bases and facilities in the Middle East that housed U.S. equipment. From April 1 to June 30 alone, the report says strikes damaged or destroyed hundreds of buildings and base infrastructure. Analysts found attackers initially targeted aircraft hangars and early‑warning systems for missile and drone detection, then expanded targets, producing equipment losses worth billions of dollars. 【券商稱韓國KOSPI被嚴重低估 建議6000點下方逐步加倉】接下來一週（11月2日–6日），韓國股市預計將受到美國中期選舉以及多項核心經濟指標公佈的影響。券商行業建議，在韓國綜合指數（KOSPI）6000點下方採取分批買入策略，稱韓國股市當前處於低估狀態。NH Investment & Securities研究員Na Jeong-hwan表示：“KOSPI指數自5000點初段位置開啟反彈，在反彈階段，應當關注放量上漲的個股；資金持續流入的標的，大機率會引領本輪反彈行情，成為領漲板塊。”券商分析指出，KOSPI預期市盈率（PER）僅4倍，已經進入極度低估區間。但機構提示，行情想要持續上漲，前提是市場對2027、2028年盈利增長形成信心，同時外資持續淨買入。 元大證券韓國（Yuanta Securities Korea）研究員Lee Jae-won稱：“KOSPI 6000點下方，可以依託股東回報收益率、市淨率（PBR）估值，在合適價位逐步加倉；同時繼續持有半導體主線標的，佈局材料、零部件、裝置（MP&E）以及儲能系統（ESS）板塊博取超額收益。”

South Korea Trade Surplus Beats Estimates — South Korea’s trade surplus widened sharply to USD 42.50 billion in October 2026, up from USD 6.00 billion in the same month a year earlier, surpassing market expectations of USD 37.24 billion and marking the 21th consecutive month of surplus. It was the second-largest on-record trade surplus, as exports continued to outpace imports, flash data showed. Exports surged 93.7% yoy, accelerating from an 83.7% surge in September, mainly driven by a surge in semiconductor shipments. Meanwhile, imports jumped 36% yoy, accelerating from a 26% rise in September, supported by resilient domestic demand.

### 2026-11-01 07:59:38  #公司

【事關金融產品網路營銷 跨區域展業、平臺營銷、API跳轉規則明確】10月31日，中國網際網路金融協會發布關於《落實〈金融產品網路營銷管理辦法〉問答(一)》的公告。

公告指出，有經營區域限制的金融機構應當結合客戶常住地、主要業務經營地、身份證地址、手機號碼歸屬地、裝置位置定位資訊等5項資訊，根據相關規定，綜合判定客戶所在區域，面向註冊地及設有分支機構區域的客戶提供金融產品。

同時，第三方網際網路平臺不得以自身名義在其他網際網路平臺上進行金融產品網路營銷。如果第三方網際網路平臺有下屬金融機構，該下屬金融機構可以自身名義在其他網際網路平臺上進行營銷宣傳，但獲得的客戶只能由該金融機構提供金融服務，不得推薦給任何第三方網際網路平臺及其他金融機構。

此外，金融消費者和投資者在第三方網際網路平臺點選申請貸款、開立證券賬戶、購買資產管理產品等購買金融產品的連結後，應當從第三方網際網路平臺跳轉至金融機構運營的網站、App、小程式、快應用、H5頁面等資訊服務載體。

公告要求，採用API（應用程式程式設計介面）模式進行跳轉的，金融機構與第三方網際網路平臺應當共同評估，並透過合作協議和技術手段確保：一是金融機構獨立運營；二是金融機構擁有完整資料許可權；三是金融消費者和投資者個人資訊保安；四是金融消費者和投資者充分知情並具備自主選擇權。

【淡水泉宏觀主題投資負責人彭皓：情緒超調開啟佈局視窗 中國核心資產有望迎來價值重估】淡水泉宏觀主題投資負責人彭皓接受上證報記者採訪時表示，儘管短期外部擾動頻發，但市場中長期發展的基石並未改變；相反，伴隨短期情緒築底，中國核心資產迎來極具吸引力的價值重估契機。彭皓表示，近期市場回撥，本質是全球宏觀風險外溢與國內情緒負反饋共振的結果。一方面，美伊地緣局勢不確定性壓制風險偏好、美債收益率高企制約估值，疊加市場對海外AI產業信用及債務風險的擔憂，導致全球資產承壓；另一方面，悲觀情緒在盤面中被放大，資金持續淨流出。當前的悲觀預期與上市公司盈利形成了鮮明背離，機構重倉股盈利在持續改善的同時，部分優質資產股價已明顯超跌。（上證報）

【中期選舉或掀藍色浪潮 共和黨將步入“後特朗普時代”】媒體民調顯示，在2024年總統選舉投選特朗普的選民中，如今約有五分之一不滿意他的執政表現，佔中期選舉選民的8%左右。無黨派選舉分析機構庫克政治報告認為，共和黨已幾乎無望在11月3日中期選舉中保住眾議院控制權。眾議院所有435席都將改選，共和黨或只能贏得198至208席，民主黨則有望翻轉12至22席，使議席總數增至227至237席。選舉新聞網站Decision Desk HQ預測，民主黨有79%的機率重奪眾議院、56%的機率控制參議院。另一個選舉預測網站ElectIt則估計，民主黨拿下參眾兩院的機率分別為93%和69%。

### 2026-11-01 07:48:47  #國際

【預測市場與民調看似分歧：二者衡量指標並不相同】本輪美國中期選舉週期內，預測市場賠率受到更多關注，頻繁出現在新聞報道、競選文稿與政治廣告的顯眼位置。有人指責Kalshi、Polymarket這類平臺曾出現判斷失誤——它們看好的候選人最終敗選。還有一部分人拿著計票過程中候選人賠率暴跌的截圖，將預測市場當作存在舞弊的證據。 儘管預測市場被包裝成“真相機器”，但它和民調一樣存在侷限性。而針對預測市場的部分反對聲音，源於人們對其能說明什麼、不能說明什麼產生了誤解。 就中期選舉而言，Kalshi與Polymarket上的賠率整體和民調方向一致。預測市場75%對25%的機率分佈，對比民調5個百分點的領先優勢，看似是一邊倒的巨大優勢，但二者大體上是吻合的。原因在於二者衡量的物件不同：民調差值體現候選人的領先幅度，而市場賠率代表候選人在選舉日勝出的可能性。對比多場關鍵中期選舉可以發現，民調基本能夠反映預測市場的整體判斷。

【金飾克價年內跌了近550元 投資黃金升溫】受國際原油價格上漲、美國通脹壓力加劇以及市場對美聯儲進一步加息的預期升溫等因素影響，國際金價10月30日跌至一年來新低。截至最新收盤，COMEX黃金期貨下跌3.63%，報3884.90美元/盎司；倫敦金現跌3.61%，報3875.19美元/盎司，雙雙跌破3900美元關口。受國際金價走低影響，國內品牌金飾價格同步下調，部分品牌金飾價格較年內高點已回落近550元/克。分析師介紹，近期受到全球多重不確定因素的影響，金價在短期內波動較為明顯，有更多的投資者來購買黃金投資金條產品。在菜百商場，有消費者購買了5塊金條，每塊金條30克，一塊約26000元。消費者表示，黃金價位較低的時候，會想做一些理財投資。（央視財經）

【專家：短期國際金價後續可能維持震盪放大的態勢】招聯首席經濟學家、上海金融與發展實驗室執行主任董希淼分析，近日金價下探，主要受市場對美聯儲利率路徑預期邊際轉鷹、美元與美債實際收益率上行、地緣避險溢價回吐、前期多頭獲利了結，以及市場風險偏好和流動性擾動等多重因素共同影響。董希淼指出，國際金價失守3900美元/盎司關口，更多具有心理和技術意義。“整數關口跌破可能引發程式化止損和趨勢資金減倉，放大短期波動，並強化市場從避險或抗通脹交易轉向利率與美元交易的敘事。”但他強調，不能據此判定長期支撐邏輯已被破壞，關鍵要看失守後能否快速收復，以及美元和實際利率是否繼續上行。他認為，短期看，國際金價後續可能維持震盪放大的態勢。“這主要取決於市場對美聯儲利率路徑的預期差、美國通脹就業資料、美元指數及美債實際收益率、地緣風險及ETF和央行購金節奏。”（中新網）

【四大行三季報出爐 利息收入集體提速，科技金融成必爭之地】翻開四大行的最新三季報，利息淨收入增速成為共同亮點，均實現了10%左右的同比增長。同時，科技金融已成為四大行的必爭之地。 農行資料顯示，今年前三季度，科技型企業、科技型中小企業貸款增速分別為28%、34%；同時，還有序推進AIC股權投資試點，累計設立試點基金33只，基金投資86個科創專案、金額73億元。加大科創債承銷投資力度，前三季度主承銷科創債49只、發行規模1346億元。 中行則指出，共為20餘萬家企業提供科技貸款支援，科技貸款餘額佔對公貸款比重超三分之一。 工行、建行未披露具體資料，不過，工行提到，科技貸款餘額市場領先；建行則表示錨定領軍銀行目標，科技貸款增速高於各項貸款平均增速，科技型企業貸款增長保持同業領先。（券商中國）

### 2026-11-01 07:34:07  #其他

【經濟日報：“電影+體育”不是簡單做加法】近日，國家電影局、體育總局聯合印發通知，啟動“跟著電影去運動”行動，推動電影與體育雙向賦能。在體驗消費、文化消費持續升溫的當下，這場跨界牽手，既是豐富大眾精神文化供給的有益探索，也是啟用文體消費新增長點的重要抓手。

熱度之上，一道考題擺在行業面前：如何跳出簡單疊加，真正打通內容、場景、人群，充分釋放文體融合的乘數效應？

首先，內容創作要沉下心，拒絕標籤化、流水線式的體育題材“速成品”。融合不等於給影片貼上體育標籤，也不是在運動專案裡生硬植入影視橋段。體育題材創作要尊重專案專業規律，紮根運動員真實的成長故事，深挖精神核心，摒棄懸浮化、娛樂化表達。唯有故事紮實、情感真摯，才能完成從“看電影”到“去運動”的轉化，內容影響力持續釋放。

其次，場景運營要算好平衡賬，兼顧商業活力與普惠屬性。不少跨界專案容易陷入短期引流的狂歡，依靠打卡噱頭、優惠活動快速集聚流量，熱度退去後便難以為繼。影院賽事直播、電影主題運動線路等新業態既要盤活閒置資源、拓展營收空間，也要守住服務底線，做好價格規範、安全保障與配套服務。面向不同群體設計差異化產品，兼顧年輕人潮流體驗、青少年體育科普、中老年休閒健身，推動文體供給從追逐網紅流量的短期熱度，轉向培育穩定持久的消費習慣。

最後，機制協同要打破部門壁壘，構建長期聯動的產業生態。電影、體育分屬不同管理體系，資源分散、活動碎片化，是跨界融合的堵點。這就需要主管部門統籌引導，打通影視機構、影院、體育場館、賽事運營方、戶外基地的對接通道，完善版權、直播、活動審批等配套規則，培育熟悉兩大領域的複合型運營主體。推動合作從單次活動走向IP共創、線路共建、品牌共推，把零散的專案升級為共生共贏的產業生態，讓優質供給觸達更多群體。

【France holds third nationwide protest in three weeks; 422 arrested】On Oct. 31 local time France held the third nationwide large-scale protest in three weeks. Interior Minister Laurent Nunez said police carried out about 40,000 security checks and arrested 422 people. The interior ministry put turnout at roughly 320,000 nationwide; student unions estimated nearly 500,000 participants, including about 200,000 in Paris. The government had classified the protests as "high risk" and deployed roughly 70,000 police and gendarmes nationwide.

### 2026-10-31 22:46:05  #MKT News

【DeepSeek publishes Super-agent design paper】Oct. 31 — DeepSeek’s Harness team published a technical paper, Super-agent design exploration: Toward more autonomous agent systems, outlining an architecture to scale agents beyond single-session limits. The paper says rising model capability has spurred personal assistant agents but current designs isolate sessions and cap multi-tasking at model ability and human attention. DeepSeek proposes a two-layer architecture with event-driven, asynchronous multi-channel interaction, compute–storage separation and an external watchdog to centralize session management and context sync, shifting humans to higher-level decision and acceptance tasks. The firm says internal Super-agent instances are already running for bug reproduction and repair, code-review responses and iterative development of DeepSeek Harness.

### 2026-10-31 22:40:36  #其他

【DeepSeek釋出全新Agent架構】DeepSeek Harness 團隊10月31日晚在官方公眾號發表技術文章《Super-agent 設計探索：邁向更自主的Agent 系統》。DeepSeek表示，模型能力提升催生了新的應用形態，個人助手（Personal Agent）開始集中湧現。然而，現有主流 Agent設計還難以在多工和團隊協作的規模上支撐這一模式，多工上限取決於人的注意力。透過雙層架構、事件驅動、非同步多通道互動、存算分離與外部 Watchdog等思路，DeepSeek團隊突破了這些限制，系統在底層接管了繁瑣的會話管理與上下文同步，使人類得以從具體的執行細節進一步上移到更高層的決策與驗收。DeepSeek把這種Agent形態及其系統架構稱為 Super-agent，文章提到，目前，基於這套思路構建的內部Super-agent例項已經在持續執行，日常參與問題復現與修復、程式碼審查響應與 DeepSeek Harness自身的迭代。當團隊成員在群聊中反饋軟體問題時，即使沒有人主動@ AI，它也能識別潛在缺陷，在後臺嘗試復現問題、尋找修復方案。 (第一財經)

【英偉達披露下一代CPO技術 原型樣品已就位】英偉達近日在SEMICON West 2026上公開展示了下一代共封裝光學（CPO）技術，名為OOI（Optics‑on‑Interposer，中介層上整合光學）。不同於現有CPO方案，OOI選擇拿下有機封裝基板，將光引擎直接整合到承載GPU的矽中介層之上，把光電轉換環節進一步向晶片核心靠攏，為下一代超大規模AI叢集提供全新互聯思路。據英偉達研究院首席研究科學家尼古拉•內多維奇（Nikola Nedovic）介紹，該方案以更大面積的中介層取代現行有機封裝基板，目前英偉達已拿到原型樣品。

【Russia says it struck Ukrainian energy and logistics infrastructure overnight】The Russian Defence Ministry said overnight Oct. 30-31 Russian forces used drones to strike Ukrainian energy and logistics infrastructure and vessels supporting Ukrainian troops. Moscow named targets including a Kyiv combined heat-and-power plant that supplied a Ukrainian logistics facility and equipment storage, a weapons-component workshop and a drone warehouse in Kharkiv, and a cargo ship south of Odesa transporting materiel. The ministry said Russian air defences intercepted and shot down 433 Ukrainian fixed-wing drones across multiple regions, including Belgorod and Samara, between 20:00 on Oct. 30 and 08:00 on Oct. 31. President Zelenskiy said Russian drone strikes damaged a CHP in Kyiv, four substations and a gas-distribution facility in Dnipropetrovsk Oblast, and a CHP in Shostka, Sumy Oblast, and hit rail infrastructure in Dnipro and Kharkiv; he said about 200,000 households in Dnipro and surrounding areas and more than 80,000 in Kyiv region lost power. The Ukrainian Air Force said Russia launched 134 drones overnight and, as of 08:30, Ukrainian air defences had destroyed 105 of them.

### 2026-10-31 21:25:56  #公司 #觀點

【張朝陽稱長影片承壓不能怪短劇】短劇持續走紅，AI短劇崛起，傳統長影片平臺的生存空間是否正在被進一步擠壓？ 10月31日，搜狐創始人、董事局主席兼執行長張朝陽在接受採訪時給出了不同的判斷。 他表示，短劇與長劇滿足的是不同的使用者需求，短劇並不會完全侵蝕長劇的市場。國內長影片平臺面臨經營壓力，不能簡單歸因於短劇的興起，更重要的原因還包括使用者付費習慣、平臺之間的激烈競爭，以及居高不下的內容製作成本。 “長影片經濟壓力的罪魁禍首不是短劇。”張朝陽表示，海外長影片平臺能夠維持經營，並不是因為短劇沒有發展起來，而是與當地使用者早年形成的高額付費習慣有關。相比之下，國內長影片平臺在激烈的使用者爭奪中，未能形成同樣程度的付費習慣，再疊加較高的內容製作成本，導致經營壓力較大。（一財）

### 2026-10-31 20:31:19  #其他

【四川華鎣大擺蕩事故調查報告公佈，兩人被移送司法】從華鎣市官網獲悉，華鎣市人民政府事故調查組釋出了華鎣市溪口瑪琉巖大擺蕩專案“5·3”一般高處墜落事故調查報告。經調查認定，該事故是一起因專案運營企業安全生產主體責任懸空、管理缺位，未有效辨識管控安全風險，違規安排未經安全生產教育和培訓合格人員上崗，屬地政府及監管部門安全監管不到位、監督檢查存在漏洞而導致的一般生產安全責任事故。2026年5月3日，華鎣市溪口鎮瑪琉巖景區1名遊客體驗大擺蕩專案時受傷，經搶救無效死亡。經調查，該事故直接原因是：大擺蕩專案操作人員未嚴格執行操作規程，未正確操作釋放繩，導致在滑動承載結構未滑至正常釋放位置時，滑動承載結構上的脫鉤器釋放裝置提前開啟，菊繩與脫鉤器分離，現場缺乏意外墜落安全防範措施，致使遊客墜落撞擊瀑布巖壁，導致事故發生。目前，2名事故直接責任人已移送司法機關處理。對在事故調查中發現的有關公職人員履職方面存在的問題，已移交紀檢監察機關追責問責。對涉事企業及其他相關人員由有關行政機關依法給予行政處罰。（新華社）

It also said Russian drones hit a Kyiv‑area thermal power plant serving a military logistics facility and equipment storage, multiple workshops producing Ukrainian weapon components, and a dry‑cargo ship supplying Ukrainian forces. The Ukrainian Air Force said Russian forces launched 134 drones from Oct. 30 18:00 to Oct. 31 08:30 and that Ukrainian air defences had shot down or suppressed 105; it said Ukrainian missile and artillery units struck one Russian command post and three drone control points and put Russian casualties at about 1,670.

【英國首相稱對為奴隸貿易正式道歉持開放態度，但排除賠償可能性】英國首相安迪·伯納姆表示，對於有關英國應就其在歷史上參與奴隸貿易一事進行正式道歉的呼籲，他持開放態度。下週，他將出席英聯邦峰會，屆時他很可能會面臨有關經濟賠償的要求。伯納姆撰文稱，英國正尋求“與那些受跨大西洋奴隸貿易影響的人們展開結構性對話”；在那場奴隸貿易中，至少有1250萬非洲人被綁架並被販賣為奴。他補充道：“傾聽有關代表英國國家進行正式道歉的呼籲，將是這一程序的重要組成部分，我對這些呼籲持開放態度。”但伯納姆拒絕了有關經濟賠償的呼籲，他寫道：“我不認為今天的英國人應當為歷史上的罪行承擔責任——無論這些罪行多麼令人髮指；畢竟，它們發生在不同的時代，受當時不同的法律管轄，且今天的英國人並未親身參與其中。”根據“跨大西洋奴隸貿易資料庫”的記錄，從15世紀到19世紀，數百萬非洲人被歐洲商人販賣為奴；其中，英國經由大西洋運送的人數估計達320萬人。

【俄稱打擊烏基礎設施 烏稱擊退俄進攻】俄羅斯國防部10月31日午間釋出的24小時戰報顯示，俄軍共打擊148處區域，目標包括烏軍使用的交通基礎設施、無人機和地面無人戰車的儲存和發射場地、彈藥倉庫，以及烏武裝部隊和外國僱傭兵臨時部署點。俄防空系統擊落604架固定翼無人機。俄方稱烏軍在各作戰方向共損失約1220人。當天早些時候，俄國防部通報稱，俄軍無人機當天凌晨擊中基輔市為軍事物流設施和武器裝備存放點供電的1座熱電廠、生產烏軍武器裝備零部件的多個車間，以及一艘為烏軍供貨的乾貨船。烏克蘭空軍當地時間10月31日通報稱，自30日18時起，俄軍出動134架無人機襲擊烏克蘭。截至31日8時30分，烏防空部隊已擊落或壓制105架俄軍無人機。過去一天，烏軍導彈部隊和炮兵部隊打擊了俄軍1個指揮所和3個無人機控制點。烏方稱俄軍死傷1670人。（央視新聞）

【加政府推動提升港口吞吐能力以降低對美出口依賴】加拿大聯邦政府10月30日宣佈，正式將該國最大港口溫哥華港羅伯茨灘2號碼頭專案列為國家利益專案，此舉旨在推動提升港口吞吐能力，以促進貿易多元化並降低對美出口依賴。據加拿大交通部當天釋出的新聞稿，該專案計劃以加強貿易基礎設施建設，降低對美國市場的依賴，推動實現到2035年將對非美市場出口額翻倍的目標。據新聞稿，該專案計劃投資59億加元（約合42億美元），將溫哥華港集裝箱吞吐能力提升約50%，每年新增240萬個集裝箱處理能力，每年可支援高達1000億加元的貿易額。加交通部表示，將其列為國家利益專案將為後續監管審批提供確定性，從而將專案商業運營時間提前至2035年，較原計劃縮短最多四年。（新華社）

### 2026-10-31 16:57:39  #其他

【《生物多樣性公約》第十七次締約方大會敦促加快落實“昆蒙框架”】聯合國《生物多樣性公約》（下稱《公約》）第十七次締約方大會（COP17）10月31日在亞美尼亞首都埃裡溫結束。大會透過了“昆明-蒙特利爾全球生物多樣性框架”（下稱“昆蒙框架”）落實情況首次全球集體進展審查、資源調動等議題的相關決定以及會議報告，敦促各方加快落實“昆蒙框架”。COP17原定30日結束。經過通宵“加時”磋商，大會於當地時間31日上午透過了全球審查決定，其中包括由主席國亞美尼亞提出、旨在加快框架落實的“埃裡溫路徑”，敦促各方加強國際合作和對發展中國家的支援，彌補目前落實框架的差距。《公約》秘書處執行秘書阿斯特麗德·肖梅克在會議結束前表示，本屆大會完成了全球審查，明確了存在的差距，現在需要沿著“埃裡溫路徑”繼續前行，以實現2030年全球恢復目標。大會還審議了金融機制、生物多樣性與氣候變化、海洋和沿海生物多樣性、森林生物多樣性等方面以及傳統知識、合成生物學和生物多樣性主流化等領域的議題。大會決定，《公約》第十八次締約方大會將於2028年在厄瓜多舉行。

【OpenAI奧特曼談記憶體短缺：全球DRAM應優先供給資料中心，閒置在玩家PC裡是浪費】OpenAI執行長薩姆・奧特曼週五在一次播客訪談中表示，在 DRAM 儲存晶片供應緊張的情況下，將記憶體集中部署在資料中心並提高利用率，才能夠更有效地利用有限的資源。奧特曼表示，從全球資源配置的角度來看，讓每個人在個人電腦（PC）裡配備大量有限的DRAM，而這些記憶體可能只有 5% 的時間在被使用，是一種“相當糟糕”的資源浪費。他主張，在全球供應鏈極度受限的環境下，應該將記憶體資源最大化地集中在雲端資料中心，以實現更高的利用率。

### 2026-10-31 15:39:07  #MKT News

【Mexico foreign ministry says U.S. state cannot unilaterally alter Mexico‑U.S. border】Mexico's foreign ministry said on the 30th the Mexico‑U.S. border cannot be changed by a decision of a U.S. state. Responding to Texas officials' claim that nine islands in the border river belong to Texas, the ministry said U.S. state governments have no authority to make such determinations. It said the International Boundary and Water Commission, the binational body authorized to implement the 1970 Mexico‑U.S. boundary treaty, has the authority to determine each island's border status, and the treaty bars either party from constructing works the commission finds could alter or impede the river's normal flow. Mexico said it will closely monitor actions in the area, adhere to the treaty and pursue bilateral dialogue through established mechanisms.

【烏克蘭國債20年間增130倍】統計資料顯示，自2006年9月以來，烏克蘭的國家內外債總額增長了130倍。烏克蘭財政部資訊簡報指出：“截至2026年9月30日，烏克蘭的國家債務和國家擔保債務為95383.5億格里夫納，合2126億美元。”據烏克蘭國家統計局資料，2006年第三季度末，烏克蘭的國家內外債總額為732.88億格里夫納。由此可知，烏克蘭國債在20年間增長了130倍。在預計預算赤字達370億美元、國債增長至2760億美元的背景下，烏克蘭明年計劃撥款1083億美元用於資助烏克蘭軍隊。與此同時，烏克蘭財政部副部長羅曼·葉爾莫雷切夫9月表示，據烏克蘭國防部計算，該國軍事開支需要更多資金，達1750億美元。基輔政權試圖透過外部融資來填補預算缺口。然而，西方撥付新一輪援助都要經過漫長的討論，合作伙伴越來越頻繁地警告說，烏克蘭必須加緊尋找自籌資金來源。

### 2026-10-31 12:20:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Slovakia #Credit Rating #Importance 0

Fitch Affirms Slovakia at ’A-’ with Stable Outlook — Fitch Ratings affirmed Slovakia’s long-term foreign-currency Issuer Default Rating at A- with a Stable Outlook on October 30, 2026, citing the country’s deep integration with the euro area and continued EU fund inflows despite mounting budget pressures. However, fiscal consolidation is expected to lose momentum ahead of the 2027 parliamentary election, with the deficit projected to widen to 5.0% of GDP in 2027 before improving from 2028. The deterioration reflects higher public-sector wages, social spending, defense expenditure, and debt-servicing costs. Still, an average debt maturity of over eight years helps contain near-term refinancing risks. GDP growth is projected to grow by 0.8% in 2026 amid higher energy prices and financing costs linked to Middle East tensions. Growth is forecast to strengthen gradually to 1.8% by 2028, on rising automotive production and stronger investment. Other major agencies keep stable outlooks, with S&P rating Slovakia A, Moody’s A3, and DBRS A (low).

【我國首次載人登月任務乘組計劃由三人組成，兩人著陸月球表面】10月31日，第七屆載人航天學術大會在北京航空航天大學舉行。記者在本屆大會上獲悉，我國計劃在2030年前實現中國人登陸月球。計劃採用了“兩次發射，一次登月”的方式實現載人登月。 中國載人航天工程副總設計師兼月球探測工程總設計師王翔在本屆大會上介紹稱，我國首次載人月球探測計劃採用環月軌道對接飛航模式。 據瞭解，這種模式將採用兩枚長征十號運載火箭，先將著陸器送往月球軌道，然後再發射載人飛船與著陸器實現交會對接，航天員進入著陸器並與載人飛船分離著陸月面，開展有關科學探測活動。完成任務後航天員乘坐著陸器從月面上升，在月球軌道上與載人飛船實現二次交會對接，航天員乘坐載人飛船返回地球。 王翔介紹稱，我國首次載人月球探測任務乘組計劃由三人組成，其中兩人著陸月球表面。 目前，中國月球探測工程登月階段任務各項研製建設工作正在紮實推進。（環球時報）

【經濟學家：日本央行加快ETF出售節奏 或為高市早苗支出計劃提供資金支援】日本央行週五宣佈，自明年1月起，按賬面價值口徑，ETF年度出售規模將從目前3300億日元提升至8600億日元（摺合55億美元）。出售提速後，預計完成全部出售所需時長將從112年縮短至約43年。依照法律規定，日本央行在扣除分紅及其他開支後，需要將盈餘收入上繳政府。出售ETF產生的收益可以提高上繳金額，為政府帶來額外非稅收入。高階經濟學家木村太郎估算，出售提速後，日本央行每年可新增約8000億日元收入。這筆潛在的額外收入，有助於高市早苗在編制下一年度預算時為其政策籌措資金。她提出的政策承諾包含為期兩年的食品消費稅減免、為期14年的增長投資計劃以及增加國防開支。 日本央行新增上繳資金，還有可能用於資助本屆政府計劃設立的經濟安全專項賬戶。瑞銀三井住友信託財富管理策略師、前內閣府官員石井和正表示：“相比於填補消費稅減免這類政策造成的大額臨時性收入缺口，日本央行上繳資金更適合用於支援跨年度專案，例如經濟安全專項賬戶。”

【China re-evaluation more than doubles rhenium resources; gold equivalent to 50 large deposits, nickel 30 — Ministry】On Oct 31, the Ministry of Natural Resources said a 2024 nationwide re-evaluation of co‑occurring and low‑grade strategic minerals found rhenium resources have more than doubled, gold resources are equivalent to adding about 50 large deposits and nickel to about 30. The ministry said it mobilized teams across 27 provinces, more than 100 units and nearly 2,000 surveyors to reassess 17 strategic minerals—including gallium, germanium, indium, chromite, nickel, cobalt, zirconium, hafnium, niobium, tantalum, vanadium, rare earths, rhenium, potash, gold, manganese and helium—completing re-evaluation at over 1,800 operating mines and medium‑to‑large mining districts and estimating the quantities of co‑occurring and low‑grade resources technically and economically recoverable under current conditions.

【China Oct PMI: manufacturing 49.3, non-manufacturing 49.7, composite 49.7】China's October manufacturing PMI fell 0.8pt to 49.3, the non-manufacturing business activity index declined 0.5pt to 49.7 and the composite output PMI dropped 1.0pt to 49.7, indicating a month-on-month cooling in activity. The manufacturing production index fell 1.9pt to 49.8 and new orders fell 1.1pt to 49.4, with both supply and demand showing slower momentum. The NBS cited demand pulled forward ahead of the Golden Week holiday and a more complex international environment as pressures on the sector. Stronger production and new orders (≥53.0) were recorded in railway/ship/aircraft/aerospace equipment and computer/communication/electronics; textiles, non-metallic mineral products and non-ferrous metal smelting/rolling registered production and new orders below the 50 threshold.

銷售價格指數為49.5%，比上月下降0.8個百分點，表明非製造業企業銷售價格總體水平有所回落。分行業看，建築業銷售價格指數為48.9%，比上月上升0.4個百分點；服務業銷售價格指數為49.6%，比上月下降1.0個百分點。
從業人員指數為46.0%，比上月上升0.1個百分點，表明非製造業企業用工景氣度略有改善。分行業看，建築業從業人員指數為42.0%，比上月下降2.3個百分點；服務業從業人員指數為46.7%，比上月上升0.5個百分點。

業務活動預期指數為54.7%，比上月下降0.8個百分點，仍高於臨界點，表明非製造業企業對市場發展保持信心。分行業看，建築業業務活動預期指數為52.3%，比上月下降3.1個百分點；服務業業務活動預期指數為55.2%，比上月下降0.3個百分點。

### 2026-10-31 09:30:17  #其他

【10月中國製造業採購經理指數公佈 景氣呈季節性回撥】國家統計局服務業調查中心、中國物流與採購聯合會今天公佈10月中國採購經理指數。資料顯示，製造業景氣水平有所回撥，製造業採購經理指數重回收縮區間，但新動能持續保持擴張，對製造業的支撐作用凸顯。

10月中國製造業採購經理指數為49.3%，較上月下降0.8個百分點，回落至榮枯線以下。從歷史資料看，每年這個時段容易受季節性因素擾動，本輪迴落主要是海外相關訂單在上月集中釋放後有所回落、長假過後消費高峰趨於平淡，疊加基礎原材料行業轉入淡季，屬於階段性變化。

10月份製造業景氣出現季節性回撥，行業分化特徵比較明顯，裝備製造、高技術製造等新動能行業延續擴張態勢，對沖了傳統行業下行帶來的壓力。

從供需來看，10月製造業市場需求整體回撥。新訂單指數49.4%，環比下降1.1個百分點；新出口訂單指數48.7%，同樣有所回落。生產指數49.8%，較上月下降1.9個百分點，生產活動總體基本平穩。

價格方面，製造業原材料購進價格、出廠價格漲勢放緩。購進價格指數56.8%，環比回落4個百分點；出廠價格指數51.7%，回落2.3個百分點。兩項指數雖有回落，但仍高於去年同期，工業品價格整體處在偏高水平。

【波蘭司法控制權爭端升級】當地時間10月30日，原定前往塞內加爾的波蘭總統納夫羅茨基途中折返回國。納夫羅茨基與總理圖斯克領導的政府之間圍繞司法控制權的爭端當天升級。當天早些時候，波蘭憲法法院網站釋出訊息稱，院長博格丹·希維恩奇科夫斯基和副院長巴爾托米耶·索哈尼斯基“被暫停履行職務一個月”。根據波蘭通訊社援引相關人士訊息報道，資深法官安娜·科爾溫-皮奧特羅夫斯卡將“暫時負責法院事務”。 不過，憲法法院官方社媒賬號對此提出異議，稱法院網站“遭到非法接管”，並強調“希維恩奇科夫斯基仍是法院院長，索哈尼斯基仍是副院長”，同時否認科爾溫-皮奧特羅夫斯卡具有憲法法院法官身份。 圖斯克對此在社媒平臺上發文稱：經歷了多年的“法律失序”後，恢復法治的過程十分艱難，有時甚至令人痛苦，需要採取果斷行動，“沒有其他道路”。 當天深夜，大批抗議者聚集在憲法法院外，現場部署了大量警力。有右翼政治人士向人群發表講話，指責圖斯克政府正在對憲法法院發動“政變”。 納夫羅茨基在社交平臺上寫道：“在這樣一個時刻，總統必須留在波蘭。我中止前往塞內加爾的行程，現在返回。” 波蘭圍繞憲法法院的政治紛爭曠日持久，已經讓這一機構近乎停擺。圖斯克政府與納夫羅茨基互不承認對方提名的法官。 （CCTV國際時訊）

### 2026-10-31 09:21:51  #MKT News

【Tsinghua’s Zhao Fuquan: Era of avoiding domestic competition by exporting overseas is over】At the Oct. 29–30 China Embraces the World automotive forum, Zhao Fuquan, director of Tsinghua University’s Institute of Automotive Industry and Technology Strategy, said tariffs, geopolitical risk, weak after‑sales networks and poor residual‑value management are increasingly constraining overseas expansion. He warned Southeast Asia is already seeing price wars and market inward‑competition, so carmakers can no longer rely on going abroad to escape domestic pressure. Zhao said globalization must prioritize global resource coordination, cross‑region platform reuse, autonomous regional profitability and two‑way innovation flows. Carmakers should establish a four‑layer global operating model—strategy, capabilities, operations and safeguards—and use new joint ventures as a key tool, adopting market‑by‑market, barrier‑sensitive strategies and shifting focus from scale expansion to profitability and sustainable growth.

【Qatar holds marathon Tehran talks; mediators to resume in Doha Saturday】Axios, citing diplomatic sources, said Qatari mediators held more than 12 hours of talks in Tehran on Friday seeking a deal to restart direct U.S.-Iran negotiations and avert a post-midterm escalation; discussions will continue in Doha on Saturday. A diplomatic source said the mediators kept regular contact with the White House and described progress as positive, though both sides remain engaged in trying to reach an agreement. Qatar is pressing Washington and Tehran to agree on a package of reciprocal measures to be implemented within days to enable a return to direct talks on Iran’s nuclear program and other issues. A U.S. official said two mutually incompatible demands have blocked progress: the Trump administration wants Iran to make nuclear concessions as a condition for resuming talks, while Iran insists the U.S. must first lift a maritime blockade, unfreeze funds and allow oil sales. Qatar is pushing Iran to permit UN nuclear inspectors access to its damaged nuclear facility as a confidence-building step; the U.S. seeks that in exchange for sanction relief.

【飛天茅臺零售均價上漲3元 青花郎兩日重跌13元創月新低 古井貢古20、習酒君品重挫】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，10月31日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價三漲八跌一平，下跌品種繼續佔據明顯優勢，累計跌幅也遠超上漲端，整體價盤連續第四日走低，再創階段性低點。上漲端，飛天茅臺上漲3元至1787元，止住此前二連跌走勢企穩；茅臺1935上漲2元至686元，止住連續兩日回撥勢頭，價格重返三週以來最高位；洋河夢之藍M6+上漲3元至603元，今日回升幅度位居上漲端前列。下跌端，精品茅臺下跌4元至2445元，昨日短暫回升後再度承壓，進一步重新整理近30天價格新低；五糧液普五八代下跌1元至791元，昨日小幅反彈未能延續，繼續執行在970元關口附近；青花汾20下跌1元至389元，昨日剛收復的390元整數位再度失守；國窖1573下跌2元至875元，四連陰持續探底，目前價格已平過去30天的最低紀錄；古井貢古20下跌6元至523元，二連陰且跌幅擴大，近期大幅震盪行情仍在持續；習酒君品下跌5元至633元，前幾日溫和回升的成果悉數回吐，再度滑向近期低點；青花郎下跌5元至675元，二連陰累計下挫13元，創下過去一個月以來的最低紀錄；水晶劍南春下跌2元至409元，連續第二日走低。持平方面，五糧液1618維持822元不變，昨日大跌後轉入橫盤，繼續停留在近一個月低位。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價繼續下探，八款產品價格下跌，其中古井貢古20重跌6元，青花郎、習酒君品均下跌5元，成為拖累整體價盤的主要品種。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10638元，較昨日下跌18元，連續第四日走低，再創今年9月中旬以來最低紀錄。

【OpenAI將融資目標下調100億美元 估值1.25萬億美元】OpenAI將最新一輪融資目標下調100億美元，正加緊敲定這筆融資。多名知情人士透露，OpenAI目前計劃募資約200億美元，估值1.25萬億美元。 公司設定截止日期，要求投資者在週五前完成出資承諾。知情人士稱，部分投資者縮減了本次融資的認購額度，本輪“首次交割”預計到位資金不足200億美元。這家ChatGPT開發商上月原本計劃募資300億美元，估值1.4萬億美元，稱這是投資者在其明年IPO前入股的最後機會。知情人士補充，由阿聯酋多家投資機構組成的財團（含國有科技基金MGX）將是本輪最大投資方。作為交易的一部分，OpenAI將獲得在該地區租賃新建算力資源的選擇權。 知情人士表示，Founders Fund、Coatue、紅杉資本、Andreessen Horowitz等風投機構，以及孫正義的軟銀也計劃參與本輪融資。OpenAI、紅杉資本、Founders Fund與軟銀均拒絕置評。MGX和Andreessen Horowitz暫未回應置評請求。

Year-to-date Apple is up about 27%; NVIDIA is down over 10% in the past month.

### 2026-10-31 05:17:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Stock Market #Importance 1

Ibovespa Posts Worst Week Since Pandemic — The Ibovespa fell 0.7% to close at 167,922 on Friday, extending its losing streak to five sessions and posting its worst weekly performance since March 2020, at the height of the pandemic. The index had rallied in early October amid bets on a change in government and fiscal adjustment. However, Lula’s reelection triggered the unwinding of those positions, while concerns over public spending and debt prolonged the selling pressure. The shift particularly affected companies sensitive to credit conditions and consumer spending. Banks declined, with Bradesco and Itaú each losing 2.1%. Steelmakers posted sharp losses, with CSN plunging 8.5%. In contrast, Vale, whose shares had plunged more than 11% over the month, rose 3.3% despite reporting a net loss in the third quarter, as its copper business helped offset costs in iron ore and nickel. Petrobras also advanced 1%, tracking oil prices. The Ibovespa sank 17.3% on the week and 10% for the month.

TSX Dips on Banks and Miners Losses — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell more than 1.7% to close at 36,620 as rebounding oil prices renewed inflation concerns. Crude rose after a tanker attack in the Strait of Hormuz underscored supply risks. Energy-driven inflation concerns have weighed on major banks since the start of the US-Iran war, raising the risk that interest rates will remain higher for longer. RBC fell 1.5%, TD Bank lost 2.3%, and BMO shed 2.9%. Meanwhile, lower gold prices pressured heavyweight miners, with Agnico Eagle down 6%, Barrick losing 4.6%, and WPM shedding 5.7%. Technology stocks also posted heavy losses amid a major selloff in US chipmakers and hyperscalers. Celestica fell 6.8%, while Shopify lost 5.7%. Elsewhere, Cameco sank 7.7% after reporting a net loss in the third quarter of 2026 despite higher revenue. Imperial Oil gained 5.4% after beating profit estimates, while Canadian National Railway shed 0.5% despite also posting strong results. The TSX lost 0.7% on the week but gained 1.5% for the month.

Wall Street Falls on Tech Slump — US stock indices closed sharply lower on Friday amid a major selloff in technology shares. The S&P 500 fell 1.8%, the Nasdaq tumbled 3.5%, and the Dow lost 572 points following disappointing tech earnings. Intel plunged 28.6% after issuing weaker-than-expected guidance. Amazon fell 1.4% after Q3 results missed expectations and it announced higher capital spending. Signs of a slowdown in AI-related business growth have sharpened scrutiny of the durability of AI demand and whether the huge sums being invested in the technology will generate sufficient returns. Among chipmakers, Nvidia fell 4.2%, AMD slumped 11%, Broadcom shed 5.5%, and Micron lost 5.3%. Among hyperscalers, Alphabet extended losses by 1.4% after a slump of 12.6%. Elsewhere, ExxonMobil gained 2.2% and Chevron advanced 1.7% as both topped Q3 forecasts. For the week, the S&P 500 fell 0.9%, the Nasdaq shed 2.4%, and the Dow lost 1.2%. For the month, the S&P 500 declined 0.9%, the Nasdaq lost 6.6%, while the Dow added 1.7%.

### 2026-10-31 02:55:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Colombia Central Bank Holds Key Rate at 12.25% — The Central Bank of Colombia left its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 12.25% at its October 2026 meeting, in line with market expectations. The Board of Directors noted that annual inflation edged up to 6.3% in September, slightly below forecasts. Core inflation, meanwhile, declined from 6.1% in August to 6.0% in September. However, the El Niño weather phenomenon represents a significant upside risk to the outlook for food and regulated prices. Inflation expectations for December 2026 edged up to 6.9%. The Board also noted that Colombia’s annual economic growth is estimated to have slowed in the third quarter compared with the previous quarter. Domestic demand indicators point to weaker consumption growth in both the private and public sectors. The Board emphasized the importance of thoroughly reviewing the Economic Rescue Bill submitted to Congress, noting that the evolution of public finances is a key factor in future monetary policy decisions.

【Pakistan military says cautiously optimistic about mediating US‑Iran de‑escalation】Spokesman Lt. Gen. Ahmed Sharif Chaudhry said on Friday Pakistan is "cautiously optimistic" about mediating a US‑Iran de‑escalation, noting Army chief General Munir visited Tehran twice this month, most recently on Monday accompanied by Saudi Defense Minister Khalid bin Salman and Turkish intelligence chief Ibrahim Kalin. Chaudhry said the repeat trips indicate some positive progress but warned powerful spoilers remain and declined to disclose meeting details or whether the US supported the visit. He said defence agreements among Pakistan, Saudi Arabia and Turkey do not preclude new members; Iran could be welcomed if it endorses collective deterrence and regional peace, but any admission would require political sign‑off by the three governments. Pakistan reaffirmed it will honour commitments to defend Saudi Arabia and the holy sites of Mecca and Medina, but said any additional troop deployments under the pact would depend on the situation and political leadership. He added Pakistan is not a party to the Yemen conflict and views it as an internal matter.

### 2026-10-31 02:24:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Uruguay #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Uruguay’s Trade Deficit Narrows in September — Uruguay’s trade deficit narrowed to $35.3 million in September 2025, from $124.4 million a year earlier. Exports grew 7.8% year-on-year to $1.14 billion, led by an 11.5% increase in manufacturing exports. Within the sector, sales of food and beverage products rose 5.8%, tobacco product exports increased 52%, and textile product sales climbed 84.4%. Exports of primary products grew 2%, as higher sales of forestry and wood extraction products (+16.5%) offset declines in other subsectors. Meanwhile, utility exports rose 2.4%. On the import side, purchases edged down 0.6% to $1.17 billion. Imports of intermediate goods recorded the largest decline, falling 5.7%, as purchases of oil and distillates dropped 53.3%. Imports of capital goods slipped 0.8%, amid an 8.4% decline in purchases of machinery and equipment. In contrast, imports of consumer goods rose 9.9%, driven by a 28.1% increase in vehicle purchases.
